### [1] [Phase-space dynamics and multicomponent wave-packet structures in the Dunkl anharmonic oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00280)
*D. Ojeda-Guillén,R. D. Mota*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究Dunkl非谐振子（Kerr介质）的相空间动力学与多分量波包结构，导出Dunkl-Husimi Q函数与归一化角向Husimi剖面的解析表达式，揭示Dunkl参数μ对相干态激发权重与宇称依赖相位积累的调控作用及相应的相空间各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Kerr介质与谐振子动力学已被充分研究，但形变（Dunkl）框架下的相空间描述尚不清晰。作者希望刻画Dunkl形变如何通过改变激发权重与宇称相关相位来重塑波包的相空间结构，从而为形变量子光学系统提供可解析的相空间诊断工具。

Method: 从时间演化态出发，解析推导Dunkl-Husimi Q函数及归一化角向Husimi剖面；分析Dunkl参数μ对相干态激发权重和宇称依赖相位积累的影响；对半整数形变μ=0.5与整数形变μ=1.0分别数值/解析考察多分量峰结构、峰高不对称性与重构行为，并检验μ→0极限。

Result: μ改变相干态激发权重与宇称相关相位积累，产生角向各向异性和不对称的相空间分离。μ=0.5时出现峰高不等的非对称多分量Husimi图样，并在t=π/λ处发生早期重构；μ=1.0时Husimi分布展宽但保持多模结构与幺正演化；在基本非线性复活时间处，所选参数比下波包完成重构；μ→0时退化为标准Kerr动力学。

Conclusion: Dunkl形变参数为调控Kerr介质中波包的多分量结构、相位空间对称性与复活/重构时标提供了有效手段，Dunkl-Husimi Q函数可作为表征此类形变系统相空间动力学的解析工具，并在极限情形下与标准Kerr结果自洽。

Abstract: We study the phase-space dynamics and multicomponent wave-packet structures of the Dunkl anharmonic oscillator (Kerr medium). Starting from the time-evolved state, we derive analytical expressions for the Dunkl-Husimi $Q$-function and a normalized angular Husimi profile. The Dunkl parameter modifies the coherent-state excitation weights and the parity-dependent phase accumulation, producing angular anisotropy and asymmetric phase-space separation. For the half-integer deformation $μ=0.5$, the dynamics generate asymmetric multicomponent Husimi patterns with unequal peak heights and an early reconstruction at $t=π/λ$. For the deformation $μ=1.0$, the Husimi distribution exhibits broadening while retaining its multimodal structure and unitary evolution. At the fundamental nonlinear revival time, the wave packet is reconstructed for the selected parameter ratio. The standard Kerr dynamics are recovered in the limit $μ\rightarrow 0$.

</details>


### [2] [Painlevé VI superintegrability and Heun-Racah algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00322)
*Ian Marquette,Sarah Post*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了极坐标下角向势由四参数第六Painlevé超越函数决定的两个四阶量子超可积系统（谐振子型与延展Coulomb型径向量），显式给出四阶量子积分、三次对称代数及其Casimir与形变振子实现，并借助Levi-Civita变换与耦合常数蜕变建立两系统的参数、代数、Casimir与表示的对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前类似构造（如例外Jacobi模型）仅适用于特殊有理参数值，本文旨在把结果推广到完整的四参数Painlevé VI族，并厘清径向量差异如何影响代数结构。

Method: 在极坐标分离变量框架下显式构造两个系统的四阶量子积分；推导三次对称代数及Casimir并给出形变振子实现；固定能量下将两代数视为Heun–Racah代数的特化，比较其结构函数；对Coulomb型积分通过算符层面变换笛卡尔厄米对称性得到，并强调坐标变换产生的低阶项对与Hamiltonian对易的必要性；用Levi-Civita变换与耦合常数蜕变联系两系统；最后与经典Poisson代数对照。

Result: 得到两系统四阶量子积分的显式表达式与三次代数结构；两代数结构函数含由Painlevé VI参数决定的相同四次角向因子，径向量子因子不同；Coulomb积分可由笛卡尔厄米对称性算符变换获得，且极坐标变换生成的低阶项对易性不可或缺；Levi-Civita变换与耦合常数蜕变给出Painlevé参数、代数、Casimir与表示的直接对应；以往例外Jacobi模型对应于特殊有理参数值。

Conclusion: 两个Painlevé VI型四阶超可积系统在量子层面被统一处理，其代数结构为Heun–Racah代数的特化；所给变换联系使结果覆盖完整四参数族，并揭示了量子与经典Poisson代数之间的对应与差异。

Abstract: We study the two fourth-order quantum superintegrable systems in the Euclidean plane that separate in polar coordinates and whose angular potentials are determined by the general four-parameter sixth Painlevé transcendent. One system has an oscillator radial term and the other an extended-Coulomb radial term. For both systems we give the fourth-order quantum integral explicitly, derive the cubic symmetry algebra and its Casimir, and construct a deformed-oscillator realization. At fixed energy the two algebras are specializations of the Heun--Racah algebra. Their structure functions contain the same quartic angular factor, determined by the Painlevé~VI parameters, while different radial factors distinguish oscillator and Coulomb dynamics. The Coulomb integral is obtained by transforming the Cartesian Hermitian symmetry at the operator level. The lower-order terms generated by the polar coordinate change are essential for commutation with the Hamiltonian. The two quantum systems are also related by the Levi-Civita transformation and coupling-constant metamorphosis, which gives a direct correspondence between their Painlevé parameters, algebras, Casimirs and representations. Earlier exceptional-Jacobi models arise at special rational parameter values, whereas the results here apply to the full four-parameter Painlevé~VI family. We also compare the quantum results with the corresponding classical Poisson algebras.

</details>


### [3] [Stability of Solitons in the Parametrically Driven and Damped Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00346)
*Fernando Carreño-Navas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究参数驱动与阻尼非线性Schrödinger方程（幂律非线性|Ψ|^{2κ}Ψ）在N维空间中孤子解的光谱稳定性，给出完整谱分析与新的不稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅在一维情形讨论PDDNLS方程孤子稳定性，高维、任意幂律非线性下的谱结构（尤其是角动量分解与复四重特征值）尚缺乏系统结论。

Method: 识别由(κ,N)与(r,ρ)两组条件决定的两族定态解Ψ±；线性化后引入ε参数化，把稳定性问题约化为两个无耗散Schrödinger型算子L0、L1的特征值问题；按角动量扇区S_l分解并作完备谱分析，结合Vakhitov–Kolokolov型判据与原点附近特征值分裂方向的局部分析导出不稳定判据。

Result: 证明Ψ−恒不稳定；Ψ+在l≥2扇区无不稳定模，S_1上不出现复四重特征值；给出S_l上的解析不稳定性判据；发现κ较小与较大时此前一维研究未涉及的新现象，并给出N=1,2,3的数值稳定性图。

Conclusion: PDDNLS方程的孤子稳定性可由约化特征值问题与角动量扇区分析完全刻画，高维与幂律非线性引入新的失稳机制，结果推广并补充了已有的一维结论。

Abstract: We study the spectral stability of soliton solutions of the parametrically driven and damped nonlinear Schrödinger (PDDNLS) equation with power-law nonlinearity $|Ψ|^{2κ}Ψ$ in $N$ dimensions. We identify two families of stationary solutions, $Ψ_\pm$, whose existence is determined by two conditions: one involving the nonlinearity exponent~$κ$ and the spatial dimension~$N$, and another involving the forcing amplitude~$r$ and the damping coefficient~$ρ$. By linearising around these solutions and introducing a convenient parametrisation through~$\varepsilon$, we reduce the stability problem to a dissipationless eigenvalue problem for two Schrödinger-type operators, $L_0$ and $L_1$. We prove that $Ψ_-$ is always unstable, while for $Ψ_+$, we carry out a complete spectral analysis by decomposing the problem into angular momentum sectors $\mathcal{S}_l$. We show that modes with $l \geq 2$ generate no instabilities and that no complex quadruplets arise on~$\mathcal{S}_1$. We also derive an analytical instability criterion on $\mathcal{S}_l$ by combining a Vakhitov--Kolokolov-type argument with a local analysis that determines the direction of the eigenvalue splitting near the origin. In addition, we uncover new phenomena at both small and large values of $κ$ that were not investigated in earlier one-dimensional studies. Numerical stability diagrams for $N = 1, 2, 3$ are presented.

</details>


### [4] [A block-encoding approach to the Segawa-Suzuki spectral mapping theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00498)
*Yohei Tanaka*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 从块编码视角重新审视Szegedy型量子游走的Segawa–Suzuki谱映射定理，用2×2块表示与Schur分解给出更简洁的证明，并统一处理谱、点谱与Fredholm本质谱及各端点本征空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Segawa–Suzuki非端点谱映射定理的证明依赖不变子空间分解与近似本征向量论证，技术复杂。作者希望保留手性对称所依赖的involution的两个本征空间，从而获得更自然、更初等的证明框架，并同时刻画端点处inherited与birth本征空间。

Method: 保留involution的两个本征空间，构造自然的两块（2×2）块表示，其对角块为通常的判别算子（discriminant operator）及其对应物；随后通过初等Schur分解导出非端点谱映射定理，替代不变子空间分解与近似本征向量论证；并将同一框架推广至谱、点谱与Fredholm本质谱，给出端点本征空间的块理论直接描述。

Result: 非端点谱映射定理由Schur分解初等得到，证明显著简化；同一块框架同时成立谱、点谱与Fredholm本质谱三种版本；端点inherited与birth本征空间获得直接的块理论刻画；并以一维split-step量子游走为例说明构造。

Conclusion: 块编码视角下的2×2块表示是理解Szegedy型量子游走谱映射的统一且初等的工具，既简化了既有证明，又自然地覆盖多种谱类型与端点本征空间，并通过一维split-step量子游走的实例加以验证。

Abstract: We revisit the Segawa--Suzuki spectral mapping theorem for abstract Szegedy-type quantum walks from a block-encoding viewpoint. Retaining both eigenspaces of the involution underlying the chiral symmetry yields a natural two-by-two block representation in which the usual discriminant operator and its counterpart form the diagonal blocks. The non-endpoint spectral mapping theorem then follows from an elementary Schur decomposition, substantially simplifying the original proof by avoiding its invariant-subspace decomposition and approximate-eigenvector arguments. The same framework establishes the result for the spectrum, point spectrum, and Fredholm essential spectrum and gives a direct block-theoretic description of the endpoint inherited and birth eigenspaces. We illustrate the construction with a one-dimensional split-step quantum walk.

</details>


### [5] [Large $N$ limit of the ratio of Lukyanov integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00662)
*Dong Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究固定实耦合 b≥1 时 Lukyanov 积分之比在 N→∞ 下的渐近性质，利用其双正交系综结构与向量 Riemann–Hilbert 方法，将对数比通过常数项（由 Fredholm 行列式表示）给出，并按粒子数奇偶分别表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lukyanov 积分之比在大 N 下的渐近行为缺乏系统刻画，而其在物理（热力学 Bethe ansatz 基态修正）与量子场论单点函数中的联系使其具有重要性。

Method: 基于 Lukyanov 积分的双正交系综结构，采用向量 Riemann–Hilbert 问题技术，在给定解析性假设下提取对数比之比的常数项，用 Fredholm 行列式表达该常数项。

Result: 在所设解析假设下得到对数比之比的渐近展开，常数项由 Fredholm 行列式刻画；展开在实参数 α 上局部一致，并分偶数与奇数粒子数两种情形给出。同时验证了物理基态 TBA 所定义修正满足这些假设。

Conclusion: Lukyanov 积分之比的渐近性可由双正交系综加 RH 方法有效处理，结果对 α 局部一致且暗示与量子场论单点函数的联系（另文讨论）。

Abstract: We study the large-$N$ asymptotics of ratios of Lukyanov integrals for fixed real coupling $b\geq1$. Our approach uses their biorthogonal ensemble structure and vector Riemann--Hilbert techniques. Under stated analytic hypotheses, we derive the logarithmic ratio through its constant term, which is expressed by a Fredholm determinant. The expansion is locally uniform in the real parameter $α$ and is formulated separately for even and odd particle numbers. We verify the hypotheses for the correction defined by the physical ground-state thermodynamic Bethe ansatz. The relation to quantum-field-theoretic one-point functions is discussed separately.

</details>


### [6] [Boosted Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Solving Homogeneous and Non-Homogeneous Lane-Emden Type Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00701)
*Luke Oluwaseye Joel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将物理信息神经网络（PINN）与集成提升（boosting）方法结合的混合框架，先由PINN求解Lane-Emden型非线性微分方程，再用回归算法（LightGBM、随机森林、XGBoost、SVR）对PINN的残差进行后处理建模并聚合，从而得到高精度修正预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINN虽将物理定律编码进损失函数，但受优化困难与逼近误差影响，预测精度与鲁棒性有限；Lane-Emden方程（恒星结构、多方气体球热行为建模）缺乏高效高精度的求解手段。

Method: 构建两阶段混合框架：第一阶段用PINN求解Lane-Emden型方程，得到初步预测；第二阶段为boosting后处理，将PINN预测的残差作为目标，采用LightGBM、随机森林、XGBoost和SVR等回归算法建模，并将各模型输出聚合以生成修正后的高保真预测。通过定量指标与对比分析同标准PINN基线系统验证。

Result: 定量指标与对比分析表明，boosting增强的PINN框架显著降低预测误差，并提升泛化能力，优于标准PINN基线。

Conclusion: PINN与提升式残差修正的结合为求解微分方程主导的科学与工程问题提供了一种强大而灵活的方法，可作为提升PINN精度的通用后处理策略。

Abstract: The Lane-Emden Equation is a class of nonlinear differential equations commonly used to model phenomena in astrophysics and mathematical physics, such as stellar structure and thermal behavior in polytropic gas spheres. This study presents a novel hybrid framework that integrates Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) with boosting methods to enhance the accuracy and robustness of solutions for Lane-Emden type differential equations. Standard PINNs leverage physical laws encoded within the loss function, but their predictive performance can be constrained by optimization challenges and approximation errors. To address this, we introduce a post-processing boosting stage where the residual errors from the PINN prediction are modeled using some regression algorithms - including LightGBM, Random Forest, XGBoost, and Support Vector Regression (SVR). The outputs from these boosted models are aggregated to generate a corrected, high-fidelity prediction. The proposed methodology is systematically validated through quantitative metrics and comparative analysis against the standard PINN baseline. The results demonstrate that the boosted PINN framework significantly reduces prediction error, and improves generalization, offering a powerful and flexible approach for solving scientific and engineering problems governed by differential equations.

</details>


### [7] [A Representation Theorem for Fractional Viscoelastic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00750)
*Andrea Giusti,Ivano Colombaro,Andrea Mentrelli,Francesco Mainardi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了由弹簧与同阶弹簧-阻尼器(spring-pot, 阶数 0<α<1)构成的有限串并联粘弹性网络的复剪切模量在合适辅助变量下为有理函数，并具有极点位于负实轴、留数为负的部分分式表示；据此得到松弛模量与蠕变柔量的有限分数阶Prony表示，从而严格证明了该类网络下修正版Mainardi–Spada猜想的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mainardi–Spada曾提出关于分数阶粘弹性模型指数（Prony型）表示的猜想，但缺乏严格证明且原表述存在偏差；同时，串并联弹簧-弹簧-阻尼器网络在流变建模中广泛使用，需要一个关于其复模量结构（极点、留数符号）与Prony级数可表示性的严格刻画。

Method: 选取恰当的辅助变量（对同阶spring-pot网络，通常为 s^α 相关的变量）将复剪切模量化为有理函数；利用网络串并联结构导致的极点-留数性质（负实轴上的单极点、负留数）进行部分分式分解；对倒数作类似分解（正留数，并允许原点处附加极点）；对相应Laplace变换求逆，得到非负幅值的有限分数阶Prony表示；并以六元件网络为例说明构造。

Result: 复剪切模量在辅助变量下为有理函数，具有限部分分式表示，极点均为负实轴上的单极点且留数为负；其倒数具有类似表示，留数为正并可能在原点多一极点；求逆后松弛模量与蠕变柔量均获得幅值非负的有限分数阶Prony表示；六元件网络示例验证了构造的有效性。

Conclusion: 对同阶(0<α<1)串并联弹簧–spring-pot网络，修正版Mainardi–Spada猜想成立并被严格证明：模量与柔量均可用有限项、非负幅值的分数阶Prony级数精确表示。

Abstract: We prove a representation theorem for finite series-parallel viscoelastic networks composed of springs and spring-pots of a common order $0<α<1$. The associated complex shear modulus, expressed in an appropriately chosen auxiliary variable, is rational and admits a finite partial-fraction representation with simple poles on the negative real axis and negative residues. Its reciprocal admits an analogous representation with positive residues and a possible additional pole at the origin. Inverting the corresponding Laplace transforms gives finite fractional Prony representations of the relaxation modulus and creep compliance with non-negative amplitudes. These results provide a rigorous proof of a corrected version of the Mainardi-Spada conjecture for this network class. A six-element network illustrates the construction.

</details>


### [8] [Lieb-Robinson Light Cones in Long-Range Bosonic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00776)
*Carla Rubiliani,Cheng Shang,Tomotaka Kuwahara*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有长程相互作用的相互作用玻色子系统中的Lieb-Robinson型光锥界，导出有效光锥指数由长程衰减指数α与玻色子相互作用阶数p的非平凡竞合所决定的上界与下界；对p≥4且2d<α≤2d+1，上下界给出的指数吻合，从而确定最优的主导光锥幂次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Lieb-Robinson界通常依赖短程相互作用与一致有界的局域能量尺度两项标准假设，而长程相互作用玻色子系统同时缺失这两点，其涌现因果结构尚不清楚，需要针对该情形建立上下界以刻画光锥传播规律。

Method: 针对长程相互作用玻色子系统，构造一种传播协议：将玻色子集中到块（blocks）内，形成具有放大相互作用的中介子晶格，从而克服无界玻色自由度带来的困难；结合有界相互作用结果的固定-k（fixed-$k$）延拓技术，导出有效的上界与下界，并分析光锥指数随α与p的变化。

Result: 所得界的有效光锥指数由α与p的竞合决定；在p≥4且2d<α≤2d+1时上下界指数一致，给出最优主导光锥幂次；上界可覆盖α=2d+1并仅带来任意小的指数损失。结果表明长程耦合与无界玻色自由度结合产生了比单纯长程有界系统或短程玻色系统更丰富的光锥结构。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用与无界玻色自由度的共同作用导致非平凡且更丰富的光锥结构，其中块集中协议与固定-k延拓是实现指数匹配的关键；该框架为刻画长程玻色多体系统的涌现因果性提供了定量基础。

Abstract: Lieb-Robinson bounds formulate emergent causality in quantum many-body dynamics. In this Letter, we study interacting bosonic systems with long-range interactions, in which both the standard assumptions of short-range interactions and uniformly bounded local energy scales are absent. We derive upper and lower bounds whose effective light-cone exponents are governed by a nontrivial interplay between the decay exponent $α$ and the bosonic interaction order $p$. The propagation protocol concentrates bosons within blocks, creating a mediating sublattice with amplified interactions. For $p\ge4$ and $2d<α\le2d+1$, the resulting exponents coincide, determining the optimal leading light-cone power. The upper bound includes $α=2d+1$ with arbitrarily small exponent loss, using a fixed-$k$ extension of the bounded-interaction result. Our results reveal that the interplay between long-range couplings and unbounded bosonic degrees of freedom gives rise to a richer light-cone structure than in either long-range bounded systems or short-range bosonic systems alone.

</details>


### [9] [Explicit Boundary-Entropy Deficits in the XX Spin Chain with arbitrary boundary fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00946)
*Yi Qiao,Pei Sun,Junpeng Cao,Wen-Li Yang,Yupeng Wang*

Result: 对每个逆温度 $\beta$ 存在 $\delta(\beta)>0$，使 $|J^i-1|\le\delta(\beta)$ 时自旋两点函数在有限子图（自由边界）与边长强度至 $\beta$ 范围内一致指数衰减。对易平面模型 $\sigma^x\sigma^x+\sigma^y\sigma^y+\lambda\sigma^z\sigma^z$，在自由边界极限下对任意 $\beta\le c|\log(1-\lambda)|^{1/2}$ 关联指数衰减，即 BKT 相起始温度随 $\lambda\uparrow1$ 趋于零，物理预测量级为 $|\log(1-\lambda)|$。结论同样适用于含约束势 $\varepsilon\sum(\sigma^z)^2$ 的对齐单离子各向异性及近各向同性 $O(n)$（$n\ge3$）模型。

Conclusion: 二维经典海森堡模型的有质量相是小各向异性稳定的；易平面与单离子各向异性模型的 BKT 相起始温度在 $\lambda\uparrow1$ 时以至少 $|\log(1-\lambda)|^{1/2}$ 的标度趋于零温度，与物理预测的 $|\log(1-\lambda)|$ 相差一个对数因子的平方根修正。

Abstract: Polyakov predicted that the classical Heisenberg model on $\mathbb{Z}^2$ has exponentially decaying correlations at every temperature, and a proof of this has recently been posted. We show that the massive phase survives small anisotropy. For every inverse temperature $β$ there is $δ(β)>0$ such that the $S^2$-valued model with couplings $\sum_iJ^iσ_x^iσ_y^i$ has exponentially decaying spin two-point functions, uniformly in finite subgraphs with free boundary conditions and in edge strengths up to $β$, whenever $|J^i-1|\leδ(β)$ for $i=1,2,3$. For the easy-plane model with couplings $σ^xσ^x+σ^yσ^y+λσ^zσ^z$ this shows that the onset of the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless phase, proved by Garban and de Montgolfier for every $λ\in(-1,1)$, moves to zero temperature as $λ\uparrow1$: in free-boundary limits, correlations decay exponentially for every $β\le c |\log(1-λ)|^{1/2}$. The physical prediction is of order $|\log(1-λ)|$. The same holds for aligned single-ion anisotropy, which includes a confining potential $\varepsilon\sum(σ^z)^2$, and for nearly isotropic $O(n)$ models with $n\ge3$. The proof keeps the sign-cluster argument of the isotropic proof, using Ginibre's correlation inequality for anisotropic planar energies, and uses the rotation symmetry only on one finite annulus, where the anisotropy is a bounded perturbation of the Hamiltonian.

</details>

Abstract: We prove a nonlinear adiabatic theorem for the repulsive Gross-Pitaevskii (GP) equation with a time-dependent trapping potential $V(x,t)$.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: This work investigates the appropriate initial condition for tsunami propagation that allows replacing a kinematic system with its corresponding static formulation, where the bottom is fixed at its final earthquake-induced deformation. For the Boussinesq model, this condition is obtained by first applying a \textit{ Boussinesq filtering operator} to the bottom profile. Furthermore, for both models, we establish an error estimate between the kinematic and static solutions that depends linearly on the earthquake rise time. This result provides a rigorous mathematical explanation for the errors observed in previous numerical tsunami simulations. As a direct consequence, the error vanishes as the bottom motion becomes instantaneous. We validate the theoretical results using an Okada solution for the seabed deformation. In particular, both the NSW model and the passive approach, which initializes the free surface with the unfiltered bottom deformation, overestimate the amplitudes of the tsunami waves relative to the linear potential theory for the configurations considered.

Abstract: We establish Anderson localisation for random capacitance operators and quantify concentration of individual modes of finite capacitance matrices for clusters of subwavelength high-contrast resonators. At weak disorder $W$, we obtain an explicit localisation window of energy width proportional to $W$; prescribed positive intervals are localised at sufficiently large disorder. A variational boundary comparison and a material-variable eigenvalue-spacing estimate transfer localisation from truncated matrices to individual modes of the finite capacitance matrix. In a bounded sample of cell size $\varepsilon$, with probability tending to one, all except for $O(\varepsilon^{1-d}\log(1/\varepsilon))$ modes in the localisation interval concentrate within radii $O(\varepsilon\log(1/\varepsilon))$. The estimates are uniform for the contrast-dependent capacitance family at small contrast. We derive the action--angle description of the resulting double-well--harmonic-oscillator Hamiltonian, obtain the quartic-well frequency map and Fourier coefficients in terms of elliptic functions, and establish monotone in-well twist. In the autonomous two-degree-of-freedom model, isoenergetic nondegeneracy supports KAM confinement on regular compact domains. Periodic electric forcing introduces a third effective frequency, although the harmonic magnetic sector leaves the unperturbed resonance loci parallel. An explicitly specified nonlinear magnetic extension produces transverse resonance intersections. We calculate leading resonant widths and local homoclinic splitting, and derive a damping-dependent splitting threshold for a stated scalar reduction. Abstract: We develop a supersymmetric spectral description of stochastic FitzHugh--Nagumo dynamics motivated by an effective antiferromagnetic neuromorphic model. A local normal-form reduction of the adopted tangential Néel-vector dynamics relates magnetic control parameters to fast activation and slow recovery coordinates. Freezing the recovery variable yields a one-dimensional Fokker--Planck equation with a quartic potential, whose similarity-transformed generator admits an exact supersymmetric factorization. The first nonzero relaxation eigenvalue is determined by the ground state of the partner operator; in the weak-noise bistable regime, it is approximated by the sum of the two interwell Kramers rates. Projecting the similarity-transformed probability density onto the two lowest modes defines a normalized auxiliary two-state amplitude. We derive its localized-basis coherence in terms of both the time-dependent basin populations and their equilibrium weights. Its maximum corresponds to equal normalized amplitude weights, which generally differ from equal basin populations. The fixed-recovery example exhibits a pronounced coherence pulse during monotonic relaxation without a spike. Conversely, the illustrated deterministic spiking trajectory repeatedly leaves the bistable domain and passes through regions with insufficient barrier separation, restricting the validity of the localized two-state approximation. These results connect stochastic relaxation spectra to an occupation-based coherence diagnostic and delimit its application to neuromorphic spiking. The diagnostic characterizes an auxiliary amplitude representation of classical stochastic dynamics and does not by itself establish microscopic quantum coherence.

### [31] [Lossless propagation bounds for long-range fermionic interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03441)

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在多项式体积增长度量空间上，具有多项式时间增长、空间衰减不损失、且空间指数阈值最优的扩展费米子多体相互作用，存在"局域代表元"，其加权相互作用范数仅随时间多项式增长；该阈值 $a>d_{\mathrm{vol}}$ 在一般情况下最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准绝热构造与量子信念传播等滤波方法需要将演化后的相互作用对滤波核积分，从而要求相互作用范数的时间增长受控。已有传播估计往往牺牲空间衰减或要求哈密顿量衰减快于被演化相互作用，本文旨在去掉这些限制并确定空间指数的最优阈值。

Method: 在体积增长阶为 $d_{\mathrm{vol}}$ 的度量空间及合适胞腔剖分上构造演化相互作用的局域代表元。对偶哈密顿量相互作用 $\Phi_u$（时间一致界）与偶相互作用 $\Psi$ 分别假设含权 $e^{\kappa|X|}(1+\mathrm{diam}X)^a$ 与 $e^{\kappa|X|}(1+\mathrm{diam}X)^p$ 的有限支撑项范数和一致有界，其中 $a>d_{\mathrm{vol}}$、$0\le p\le a$、$\kappa$ 充分大；进而证明局域代表元的相互作用范数以权 $e^{c(1+|t-s|)^{-b}|X|}(1+\mathrm{diam}X)^p$ 加权时随 $|t-s|$ 至多多项式增长（$c,b>0$ 适当）。

Result: 允许 $p=a$，即哈密顿量无需比被演化相互作用衰减更快。阈值 $a>d_{\mathrm{vol}}$ 一般最优：当 $0<a\le d_{\mathrm{vol}}$ 时，即使对光滑含时两体相互作用，$p=a$ 处的一致多项式时间界亦失效。多项式时间增长可与具有足够多有限绝对矩的滤波核积分，据此给出滤波相互作用及其生成动力学的估计，包括能隙稳定性、局域扰动局域作用（LPPL）、绝热理论与响应理论；结果对量子自旋系统同样成立。

Conclusion: 建立了费米子（及自旋）多体系统演化相互作用的传播估计，本论文刻画了空间指数阈值的最优性与时间多项式增长的兼容性，为扩展扰动微扰论、准绝热与量子信念传播等应用提供了统一动力框架。

Abstract: We prove propagation estimates for extensive fermionic many-body interactions with polynomial time growth, no loss of spatial decay, and an optimal threshold for the polynomial spatial exponent. Here $a>d_{\mathrm{vol}}$, $0\le p\le a$, $κ$ is sufficiently large, and the Hamiltonian bound is uniform in time. We construct local representatives whose interaction norms with weight $e^{c(1+|t-s|)^{-b}|X|}(1+\operatorname{diam}X)^p$ grow at most polynomially in $|t-s|$, for suitable $c,b>0$. In particular, $p=a$ is allowed: the Hamiltonian need not decay faster than the interaction being evolved. The threshold $a>d_{\mathrm{vol}}$ is optimal in general: uniform polynomial time bounds at $p=a$ fail for $0<a\le d_{\mathrm{vol}}$, even for smooth time-dependent two-site interactions.
  Polynomial time growth permits integration against filters with sufficiently many finite absolute moments, including those used in quasi-adiabatic constructions and quantum belief propagation. We derive consequences for filtered interactions and the dynamics they generate, providing dynamical estimates for perturbation theory with extensive perturbations, including gap stability, the principle that local perturbations perturb locally (LPPL), adiabatic theory, and response theory.
  All results also hold for quantum spin systems.

</details>

This is of interest as the spacetimes possess flat geometry but non-trivial topology. We obtain some quantum interference effects, due to the superposition of spacetimes, which would be absent if a classical probabilistic mixture of spacetimes would be considered. In this manner, we obtain real physical consequences that would be indications of a Quantum Gravity phenomena.

The Hamiltonian formulation is constructed by following the appropriate methods to approach higher-order systems. The constraint structure is identified for several cases that arise depending on imposed conditions on the values of the function $f(R)$ and its derivatives, classification of constraints allows to make a proper count of degrees of freedom for each case, at the end we confirm the spectrum behaviour of $f(R)$ gravity around Minkowski spacetime that has been reported by another means in the recent literature.

The s-wave thresholds are zero-energy resonances at which the growth rate rises linearly, whereas the $ \ell \geq1\ $ threshold states are square integrable and the growth rate rises as a square root. At the first threshold we also obtain the next-order term, $ σa=(π/4)g-(3π/16)g^2+O(g^3) $ with $g=2ζ-1 $. Numerical evolutions are consistent with saturation on the nodeless branch. These nonlinear results refer to the probe-field model; they do not establish the existence or stability of fully backreacted scalarized wormholes.

Abstract: We numerically investigate the interior dynamics of $D$ dimensional Reissner-Nordström (RN) black holes perturbed by a real scalar field. The Schwarzschild limit yields $\Sexc=πh_c^2$, while $\Sexc=0$ at the Kerr endpoint. The Kerr reference entropy satisfies $S_+^{\Kerr}+S_-^{\Kerr}=4πM^2$, so rotation redistributes its two-horizon contributions at fixed mass. For the time-dependent model, we derive exact evolution equations for the inner root and $\Sexc$, interpreted as root-bookkeeping identities unless the roots are established as dynamical horizons. In the minimal $N=1$ trajectory, the apparent pole at $n=2$ is removable in the mass profile, giving a logarithmic continuation of the root equation, although the stationary curvature is singular there. The stationary Ricci-scalar density is locally absolutely integrable for $-1<n\leq2$, with the Kerr endpoint $n=-1$ treated separately. Minkowski breaking occurs at $n=0$, independently of inner-horizon disappearance. Abstract: The dimensionality of spacetime is a fundamental property of nature, yet whether our observed four-dimensional spacetime is embedded in a higher-dimensional geometry remains experimentally unresolved. Gravitational waves (GWs) offer a direct probe of compact extra dimensions through the strong-field dynamics of binary coalescences. In this work, we investigate extra-dimensional modifications to GW generation within a compactified Kaluza--Klein framework. We derive the Newtonian conservative binary dynamics and the leading-order extra-dimensional correction to the GW energy flux and formulate the corresponding waveform modifications within the parameterized post-Einsteinian (ppE) framework. Combining these results with the previously derived 1PN amplitude correction arising from perturbations of the internal volume, we construct modified GW waveform templates for ground- and space-based detectors. To alleviate parameter degeneracies in GW parameter estimation, we combine the observed strain data of GW170817 with independent electromagnetic observations in a multimessenger Bayesian analysis, yielding a 95\% credible upper bound of $n<0.11$ on the phenomenological dimensional parameter. We further investigate injected multimessenger massive black hole binary observations with Taiji, finding that the upper bound could be improved by nearly two orders of magnitude to $n<0.00178$. Abstract: The Moon provides a promising environment for gravitational-wave (GW) observations in the midband. The Laser Interferometer Lunar Antenna (LILA) combines a lunar deformation channel (LDC) and a geodesic deformation channel (GDC) to cover the midband. Aluminum-equivalent shielding from 50 to 1000 mils (1.27 to 25.4 mm) and two electrostatic actuator voltage configurations are compared. In the uniform-charge approximation, balanced drive with electrode configuration (B) substantially reduces linear charge-to-force coupling. Ideal continuous compensation of the mean charging current suppresses coherent charge accumulation while introducing negligible discharge shot noise. With compensation, charge-related noise dominates the low-frequency response, whereas thermoelastic deformation dominates at higher frequencies. Accumulated-dose and photodetector transient estimates identify additional component-qualification considerations. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟数据（mock data）挑战需要长段、具有指定谱的有色高斯噪声。标准引力波软件库默认在分析频带边缘直接截断目标谱，导致生成噪声与目标协方差显著偏离；同时不同生成器在精度、吞吐、状态占用与可复现性上缺乏统一的评判口径。本文旨在给出一个可比较的、基于代数计算的统一误差度量，厘清各候选方法的精度—吞吐权衡，并给出实用的生成方案与目标谱处理建议。

Method: 候选方法包括：标准引力波库实现的独立窗口交叉淡化、overlap-save 有限冲激响应滤波、自回归（AR）与 ARMA 模型、以及 PyCBC 的可复现滤波器。核心度量是从各生成器的代数（线性算子/协方差结构）直接计算的、分析频带内相对目标谱的协方差误差，生成噪声仅用于检验实现是否正确。另测吞吐量、保留状态大小与位精确重生成的代价。并对比目标谱两种处理方式：在带下平滑延拓与在带边缘直接截断；同时把交叉淡化的误差分解为循环平稳（cyclic）分量与平稳分量。

Result: 带下平滑延拓时，窗长 ≥256 s 的交叉淡化在按表列出的全部 11 条探测器曲线上误差低于 10⁻³，吞吐约 4.7×10⁶ 样本/秒，状态仅数百字节。达到 10⁻³ 误差所需窗长为 8–235 s，最长出现在含窄线的设计曲线上，且与谱表列方式有关。overlap-save 滤波吞吐更高，但需数万抽头才能把误差降到百分之几；所有测试的 AR 与 ARMA 配置均不落在精度—吞吐前沿上。若目标谱在带边缘直接截断（当前生成器默认做法），所有方法在投影的 aLIGO O4 曲线上误差均超过 1/2，交叉淡化在另外五条曲线上也超过 1/2。交叉淡化是循环平稳过程；带下平滑延拓时，循环分量仅在 8 s 窗、a LIGO O4 与 Virgo、KAGRA 设计曲线上成为误差的主导部分，并在 aLIGO O3 与 Virgo、KAGRA 设计曲线上保持在平稳分量两倍以内。

Abstract: Mock data challenges need long stretches of colored Gaussian noise with a prescribed spectrum. The candidates are the crossfade of independently drawn windows that the standard gravitational-wave libraries implement, overlap-save finite-impulse-response filtering, autoregressive and autoregressive moving-average (ARMA) models, and the reproducible filter of PyCBC. One measure ranks them all: the covariance error against the target over the analysis band, evaluated from each generator's algebra; generated noise only checks the implementations. We also measure throughput, retained state and the cost of bit-exact regeneration. With the target continued smoothly below the band, the crossfade with windows of 256 s or more reaches an error below $10^{-3}$ on all eleven detector curves as tabulated, at about 4.7 million samples per second from a state of a few hundred bytes. An overlap-save filter is faster but needs tens of thousands of taps for an error of a few percent, and no tested autoregressive or ARMA configuration lies on the accuracy--throughput fronts. An error of $10^{-3}$ needs a window of 8 to 235 s, longest on the design curves with narrow lines, where it also depends on the tabulation. With the target cut at the band edge, as current generators do by default, every method has an error above one half on the projected Advanced LIGO (aLIGO) O4 curve, and the crossfade on five other curves. The crossfade is cyclostationary. With the continued target, its cyclic part is the larger part of the error only at 8 s, on the aLIGO O4 and the Virgo and KAGRA design curves, and stays within a factor of two of the stationary part on the aLIGO O3 and the Virgo and KAGRA design curves. We recommend the crossfade with a window from the table of detector curves, and a smooth continuation of the target below the band.

</details>

Abstract: We construct a new family of exact spherically symmetric solutions in $D$-dimensional Brans-Dicke theory with $n$ internal Ricci-flat spaces. By performing a conformal transformation to the Einstein frame, the problem is reduced to Einstein gravity with a minimally coupled scalar field, for which the exact solution is already known. Applying the inverse transformation to the Jordan frame yields explicit expressions for the metric and the Brans-Dicke scalar field, together with a generalized algebraic constraint on the exponents. The obtained solutions generalize the four-dimensional Brans solutions and the multidimensional solutions with Ricci-flat internal spaces. We analyze the causal structure of the four-dimensional section and derive the necessary condition for the surface $R=L$ to be an event horizon, $ν\equiv \left|a+b+\frac12\sum_i a_iN_i\right| \ge 1$. A Kretschmann-scalar analysis shows that, apart from the Schwarzschild limit and the case of frozen internal spaces, a regular horizon is generically absent when the extra dimensions are dynamical. For the four-dimensional section we also obtain the photon-sphere and ISCO radii and discuss their Schwarzschild limits. The results clarify the role of nonminimal Brans-Dicke coupling in multidimensional black-hole geometries with Ricci-flat internal factors.

Abstract: In this work we propose a holographic dark energy model inspired by the Ricci scalar $R$ and non-metricity scalar $Q$. The infrared cutoff of the holographic dark energy is described by a combination of the Ricci scalar and the non-metricity scalar. Such a structure is very helpful because the evolution is entirely dependent on the universe's current characteristics rather than its previous or future characteristics. Additionally, the theory becomes more fundamental due to the application of invariants. We have built the model and examined its cosmological characteristics. The behavior of several cosmological parameters, including the density parameter, equation of state parameter, and deceleration parameter, is examined. It is observed that the holographic dark energy model may depict every cosmological epoch, beginning with radiation in the early universe, moving on to matter, and concluding with the dark energy-dominated period in later times. Depending on the parameter spaces selected, the equation of state parameter demonstrates that the model may have quintessence nature, phantom-divide crossing, or even phantom nature in future times.

Thus, the quantum properties of the emitted particle do not alter the global topological class, but they reorganize the local defect and bifurcation structure within it. For every $F$, $κ$ and potential this metric is conformally flat, so torsion acts only through the Einstein-frame kinetic function $K(φ)$. In the Jordan frame the bounce at the symmetric event is independent of torsion and persists only for $ξ<ξ_c$ (closed form); torsion changes the late-time exponent from $1-\sqrt{6ξ/(1+6ξ)}$ to $1-\sqrt{6ξ}$. For exponential potentials, exact Wheeler-DeWitt solutions show that in the sector containing the ekpyrotic attractor no Bohmian trajectory bounces, and single-momentum states bounce only where the potential is negligible and end in the crunch. For quadratic inflation the torsion-specific shift of $r$ is below one per cent. A single ekpyrotic field gives blue spectra, $n_s=3+2/(ε-1)$ and $r=16ε$, whatever the torsion, also for running $ε$. In an entropic two-field version the tilt shifts at order $\sqrtξ$ (non-minimal coupling) and $ξ^{3/2}$ (torsion). Tensor modes cross the massless Bohmian bounce almost unchanged and stay blue.

Abstract: We develop and validate a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework for computing the tidal Love numbers of black holes directly from their perturbation equations. The framework combines exact enforcement of the horizon boundary conditions with an $H^2$-Sobolev loss that incorporates derivatives of the differential-equation residual. The Love number is then obtained by extracting the asymptotic coefficients of the learned solution, without using the analytic connection formula. Across the tested scalar, vector, and tensor perturbations, the method recovers the finite dissipative response with relative errors below $10^{-5}$. The conservative response, which vanishes analytically, is constrained by the numerical error floor. These results demonstrate that PINNs can accurately compute black-hole tidal response and establish a validated framework for future extensions to frequency-dependent and non-separable perturbation problems.

Abstract: The static interaction potential of two non-spinning point masses in general relativity was previously computed through seventh post-Newtonian order using methods of effective field theory. Abstract: Measurements of the entropy and temperature distribution of the intracluster medium (ICM) of local clusters have proven pivotal for the study of cluster astrophysics. Here, we extend these measurements to $z=1.7$ with a resolved analysis of the ICM in ACT/SPT-CL J0459.6-4946. By combining high-resolution thermal Sunyaev-Zeldovich measurements using new, deep ALMA Band 1 and 3 observations, with archival X-ray data, we conclude that the cluster has (i) an ICM with a flat and relatively high ($\approx 200$ keV cm$^2$) entropy distribution out to $\approx 0.3\,r_{500,\mathrm{c}}$, (ii) a hot core ($T_{\mathrm{e}} \simeq 14$ keV) with a declining temperature profile, (iii) a short effective path length along the line of sight of $l_{\mathrm{eff}} \simeq (0.75 \pm 0.05) \times r_{500,\mathrm{c}}$, and (iv) a clumpiness factor $C = \langle n_{\mathrm{e}}^2 \rangle / \langle n_{\mathrm{e}} \rangle^2 \simeq 1.17 \pm 0.05$ within $r_{500,\mathrm{c}}$. Its dynamic nature and high redshift suggest that the cluster is still thermalising its ICM, consistent with a relatively large hydrostatic mass bias of $b = 1 - M_{500,\mathrm{c,HE}}/M_{500,\mathrm{c,WL}} = 0.27^{+0.32}_{-0.67}$ that we measure with respect to the weak lensing mass. Furthermore, a region with low entropy and high density to the south of the cluster centre is observed in the X-rays yet absent in the Compton-$y$ map, indicating pressure equilibrium; we interpret this as a sloshing cold front -- the first such detection at high redshifts. This case study demonstrates that $\approx 3$h-long ALMA observations, combined with comparatively "short" X-ray exposures ($\approx 122$ ks), can fully characterise the ICM at high redshift, opening a path towards the thermodynamics of young and distant galaxy clusters for larger samples.

Abstract: Environmental density correlates with galaxy properties in the local Universe, but how and when these trends emerge is unclear: at cosmic noon, large-scale structures are still assembling and galaxies quench rapidly for debated reasons. We study how the formation and quenching histories of massive galaxies at $1.7<z<3.5$ depend on environment, using two independent tracers: galaxy number density ($δ_\#$) from COSMOS-Web and intergalactic neutral hydrogen ($δ_{HI}$, cool gas) from LATIS Ly$α$ tomography. We combine 139 Blue Jay JWST/NIRSpec galaxies with 57 DAWN JWST Archive prism spectra in COSMOS, deriving stellar masses, star formation histories, dust attenuation, and Na\,{\sc d} outflow rates from joint spectro-photometric \texttt{Prospector} fits. In massive quiescent galaxies, the formation-to-quenching interval increases with density. AGN and Na\,{\sc d} outflows are confined to $δ_\#<0.5$ and elevated Ly$α$ absorption ($δ_{HI}>1$), mostly in massive quiescent galaxies showing both. About 65\% of the sample follows a correlation between the tracers; two outlier regions stand out. A galaxy-rich, gas-poor region ($δ_\#>2$, $δ_{HI}<1$) coincides with one of the most massive Hyperion protocluster cores, where the gas is plausibly fully ionized; its quiescent galaxies formed earlier and lack significant AGN activity and outflows. A galaxy-poor, gas-rich region ($δ_\#<1$, $δ_{HI}>1$), within 60 cMpc of the densest Hyperion cores, hosts rapidly quenched galaxies with a high incidence of AGN and outflows. This \emph{developing environment}, distinct from both mature protoclusters and the field, likely traces massive halos still assembling. Galaxy counts alone cannot capture the environment at cosmic noon: jointly mapping galaxies and cool gas is crucial to understand how environment regulates gas accretion, star formation, and quenching.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小红点(LRDs)是新发现的群体，可能追踪快速生长的黑洞并连接种子黑洞与明亮AGN。但此前没有任何LRD在z>10被光谱确认，这一最早阶段在观测上尚未被探索。

Method: 基于EMBER项目的JWST/NIRSpec光谱，测量目标的光谱红移。红移由显著的Lyα断裂和多个暂定发射线(包括C IV λλ1549,1551和[O III] λ4363，S/N分别为3.9和3.6)限定。并结合NIRCam-MIRI极端颜色(m_F444W-m_F770W=1.82±0.1)、近巴尔末极限转折的V形连续谱、连续谱斜率β_UV=-2.26与β_opt=1.17进行判定。

Conclusion: 研究将黑洞增长的观测前沿延伸至种子黑洞形成时代，未来MIRI光谱将进一步确立该源的物理本质。

Abstract: Little red dots (LRDs) are a newly discovered population that may trace rapidly growing black holes (BHs) and provide a link between seed BHs and luminous active galactic nuclei (AGNs). However, no LRD had previously been spectroscopically confirmed at $z>10$, leaving this earliest phase observationally unexplored. Using JWST/NIRSpec spectroscopy from the EMBER program, we measure a spectroscopic redshift of $z_{\rm spec}=10.361^{+0.003}_{-0.002}$ for \target, the highest-redshift LRD candidate known to date. The redshift is constrained by a prominent Ly$α$ break and multiple tentative emission lines, including C\,{\sc iv}\,$λ\lambda1549,1551$ and [O\,{\sc iii}]\,$\lambda4363$ detected at $S/N=3.9$ and $3.6$, respectively. Its extreme NIRCam-MIRI color of $m_{\rm F444W}-m_{\rm F770W}=1.82\pm0.1$, characteristic V-shaped continuum with a turnover near the Balmer limit, and continuum slopes of $β_{\rm UV}=-2.26^{+0.11}_{-0.11}$ and $β_{\rm opt}=1.17^{+0.74}_{-0.70}$ all support its LRD nature. A possible iron complex at $λ_{\rm rest}\sim0.24$--$0.25\,{\rm μm}$ and a possibly high $\log\left([{\rm O\,III]\,\lambda4363/\rm Hγ}\right)=0.17^{+0.24}_{-0.22}$ further support an AGN-like interpretation, as seen in previously known LRDs. Tentative detection of Ly$α$ emission may indicate enhanced Ly$α$ transmission at $z\sim10$. Our findings extend the observational frontier of BH growth into the seed-BH formation era, and upcoming MIRI spectroscopy will further establish the physical nature of the source.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST 将吸积黑洞的搜索拓展至高红移、低质量区间，为检验黑洞种子形成与早期黑洞-星系协同演化（M_BH–M_⋆ 关系）提供关键样本。

Conclusion: MARTA 3887 是一个可同时研究黑洞成长、恒星组装与电离光子逃逸的低质量高红移实验室；其超出的 M_BH/M_⋆ 提示黑洞成长先于宿主可见质量的组装。

Abstract: JWST has extended the search for accreting black holes into the high-redshift, low-mass regime. We report a low-mass black hole candidate in MARTA 3887, a clumpy, metal-poor galaxy at $z=3.7694$, combining deep JWST/NIRSpec spectroscopy, HST and JWST imaging, and archival VLT/MUSE data. We detect a broad H$α$ component with $L_{\rm Hα,b}=2.35\times10^{41}$ erg s$^{-1}$ and FWHM $=1270^{+260}_{-250}$ km s$^{-1}$, with no [O III] counterpart of comparable width. Under a broad-line-region interpretation, virial calibrations give $\log(M_{\rm BH}/M_\odot)=6.52^{+0.18}_{-0.23}$ and $λ_{\rm Edd}=0.07$, or up to 0.38 for alternative calibrations. The host comprises at least three compact clumps, with $\log(M_\star/M_\odot)=8.16^{+0.09}_{-0.10}$. If stellar photoionization dominates the narrow lines, the gas is highly ionized and metal poor, with $12+\log({\rm O/H})=7.68\pm0.04$ ($\simeq0.10\,Z_\odot$). The inferred $M_{\rm BH}/M_\star=2.3\%$ lies about 2 dex above the extrapolated local relation, suggesting that black-hole growth has outpaced the assembly of the observed host. If growth began only when the first 10% of the observed stellar mass had formed ($t_{10}=92$ Myr ago), a $10^2\,M_\odot$ seed would require a lifetime-mean Eddington ratio $\simeq5.7$; earlier growth or a more massive, e.g. direct-collapse, seed would allow sub-Eddington mean accretion. MUSE reveals extended Ly$α$ emission ($\simeq6.5$-6.9 proper kpc), offset by $+64^{+10}_{-8}$ km s$^{-1}$ from systemic, with a provisional escape fraction of $\simeq0.44$. Together with a Mg II doublet ratio close to the optically thin limit and a high [O III]/[O II] ratio, this makes MARTA 3887 a laboratory to study black-hole growth, stellar assembly, and ionizing-photon escape jointly.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRD是JWST发现的一类新型早期宇宙天体,具有V形光谱和极端致密结构,但其物理本质(黑洞还是致密星团)仍存激烈争议。作者希望借助引力透镜放大效应,突破分辨率与灵敏度限制,直接探测LRD的内部结构与中心引擎尺度。

Method: 对z_spec=8.297±0.002的引力透镜LRD"Best Friend"进行JWST观测,利用高放大率(μ=36^{+8}_{-12})解析宿主结构与中心引擎;分析其光谱(未探测到宽线)以判别黑洞活动或致密星团;并通过NIRCam测光与NIRSpec/PRISM光谱的空间分辨分析估算宿主恒星质量与恒星密度下限。

Result: 中心引擎尺度小于7 pc;光谱更适合用黑洞活动而非致密星团解释;若按星团解释,恒星密度下限为1.25^{+0.4}_{-0.3}×10^6 M⊙/pc²,比高红移典型星团高约两个数量级;宿主恒星质量log(M⋆/M⊙)=7.3^{+0.2}_{-0.8}。

Conclusion: "Best Friend"证明了引力透镜在揭示LRD形态结构方面的强大能力,为检验LRD的黑洞活动假说提供了关键观测证据,并给出其宿主星系的恒星质量约束。

Abstract: Little red dots (LRDs) are an intriguing new class of sources in the early universe discovered by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) characterized by V-shaped spectra and extremely compact sizes. Their underlying mechanisms still remain the subject of ongoing debate. We present JWST observations of a gravitationally lensed LRD nicknamed "Best Friend" at $z_{\rm spec} = 8.297\pm0.002$. Its high magnification factor of $μ= 36^{+8}_{-12}$ makes it the highest magnified LRD reported to date and unveils both the resolved structure of its host and a central engine less than 7 pc in size. The spectrum of the central engine, in which broad lines are not detected, possibly owing to the limited S/N and spectral resolution, is better interpreted as black hole activity rather than a dense star cluster. The latter cannot be fully excluded, but if true, the resulting lower limit on stellar density would be $>1.25^{+0.4}_{-0.3}\cdot10^6 \mathrm{M_{\odot}/pc}^2$, which is around 2 orders of magnitude higher than stellar densities of typical clusters at high-z. By performing a spatially resolved analysis of NIRCam photometry and NIRSpec/PRISM spectroscopy, we find that the host stellar mass is $\log(M_\star/M_{\odot}) = 7.3^{+0.2}_{-0.8}$. Best Friend demonstrates the power of lensing to reveal the morphologies of LRDs.

</details>

TL;DR: 经典密度波理论预言的蓝→红年龄–颜色梯度可被尘埃消光逆转，在光学厚度较高区域产生表观的红→蓝"颜色跳变"，故该现象不必然与密度波图像矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测显示旋臂不同波段俯仰角存在红→蓝"颜色跳变"趋势，与经典密度波预言的蓝→红（激波诱导恒星形成导致）年龄–颜色梯度方向相反，这一表观矛盾来源不明。

Method: 考察尘埃消光对激波后恒星辐射的影响，并结合气体与星族的流线（streamline）运动，分析不同波段恒星辐射流量极大值的相对位置变化。

Result: 在光学厚度 \hatτ_V ≳ 0.2 的较高消光区域，尘埃消光可反转不同波段流量极大值的相对位置，从而产生相对于激波位置的表观红→蓝空间序列；流线运动使激波后星族在旋臂附近滞留时间较圆轨道模型更长，进而改变辐射光的空间分布。

Conclusion: 表观红→蓝颜色跳变并不必然否定经典密度波场景；它可由同一批激波后星族在尘埃消光改造下呈现，而这些星族本征上产生的是蓝→红年龄–颜色梯度。

Abstract: The classical density-wave scenario predicts a blue-to-red age--colour gradient across the spiral arms of disc galaxies as a consequence of shock-induced star formation. However, observations of spiral-arm pitch angles at different wavelengths sometimes suggest the opposite trend, namely a red-to-blue ``colour jump''. The origin of this apparent contradiction remains unclear. In this work, we investigate the effects of dust attenuation on post-shock stellar emission, together with the streamline motions of gas and stellar populations. We find that dust attenuation in regions with relatively high optical depths, $\hatτ_{V} \gtrsim 0.2$, can produce an apparent red-to-blue spatial sequence, with respect to the shock location, by reversing the relative positions of the flux maxima of stellar populations observed in different bands. Moreover, streamline motions confine post-shock stellar populations near the spiral arms for longer than in a circular-orbit model, thereby modifying the spatial distribution of the emitted light. We conclude that the apparent red-to-blue colour jump does not necessarily contradict the classical density-wave scenario. Instead, it can arise from the same post-shock stellar populations that intrinsically produce a blue-to-red age--colour gradient if their emission is modified by dust attenuation.

</details>

*Stefania Barsanti,Andrew J. Battisti,Sabine Bellstedt,Matthew Colless,Scott M. Croom,Caroline Foster,Katherine E. Harborne,Claudia D. P. Lagos,J. Trevor Mendel,Matt S. Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 基于MAGPI巡天（0.20<z<0.42），利用解析恒星与电离气体运动学重建三维自旋轴，测得星系自旋-纤维结构对齐信号在恒星星系质量log(M*/Msun)~10.8处发生翻转：低质量星系自旋倾向于平行于宿主纤维，高质量星系倾向于垂直。该转变质量比z~0.05的SAMI巡天高约0.6 dex（99.5%置信度），表明过去约3 Gyr内自旋-纤维对齐关系发生了演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往自旋-纤维对齐研究多依赖低红移（z~0）样本和二维投影或单成分运动学，难以区分恒星与气体成分、也难以追踪红移演化。本文旨在利用更高红移、具备解析运动学的MAGPI数据，检验自旋-纤维对齐信号随宇宙时间的演化，并厘清气体与恒星成分在大尺度结构耦合上的差异。

Method: 使用MAGPI巡天0.20<z<0.42的星系样本，借助解析的恒星与电离气体运动学重建三维自旋轴；利用质量匹配的常数量密度星系分布（融合GAMA、MAGPI Redshift Survey与DESI数据）识别宇宙纤维结构；比较不同恒星质量下的对齐信号，并与z~0.05的SAMI巡天进行对照，通过bootstrap分析评估转变质量差异的显著性。

Result: 在z~0.3探测到对齐信号随M*的翻转：低质量星系自旋偏好平行于纤维，高质量星系偏好垂直；转变质量log(M*,t/Msun)~10.8，比SAMI高约0.6 dex（bootstrap给出99.5%置信度）。在固定恒星质量下，MAGPI星系比SAMI对应星系表现出更强的平行对齐。MAGPI中气体成分相对质量匹配的SAMI样本与纤维的对齐更紧密，说明z~0.3时电离气体与大尺度结构耦合更强，与纤维上连贯气体吸积在高M*下仍保持有效相符。在z~0处则在更低质量处出现向垂直对齐的转变，且气体与恒星对齐程度相近，可能源于并合与长期演化（secular evolution）的累积效应。

Conclusion: 星系自旋-纤维对齐关系并非静态，而是在约3 Gyr内发生演化：转变质量向高质量端移动、平行对齐整体增强、气体-纤维耦合更为显著。这支持气体吸积在较高红移下对自旋取向塑造更有效的图像；至z~0，并合与内部演化过程逐渐削弱气体与大尺度结构的关联，使气体与恒星成分趋于一致。

Abstract: We present measurements of galaxy spin-filament alignments at 0.20<z<0.42 using the MAGPI Survey, exploiting resolved stellar and ionised gas kinematics to reconstruct 3D spin axes. Cosmic filaments are identified using a stellar mass-matched constant-number-density population from the GAMA, MAGPI Redshift Survey and DESI datasets. We detect a flipping of the statistical alignment signal at z~0.3 as a function of M*: low-mass galaxies preferentially exhibit spins parallel to the direction of their host filaments, while high-mass galaxies tend towards perpendicular orientations. The transition mass is measured to be log(M{*,t}/Msun)~10.8, approximately 0.6 dex higher than the value for the SAMI Galaxy Survey at z~0.05. Bootstrap analysis gives 99.5% confidence that the transition mass is higher for MAGPI than SAMI. At fixed stellar mass MAGPI galaxies exhibit stronger parallel alignments than their SAMI counterparts, suggesting evolution of spin-filament alignments over the past ~3 Gyr. The gaseous component in MAGPI galaxies is more closely aligned with filaments than the stellar component, relative to a M*-matched SAMI sample. This indicates a stronger coupling between ionised gas and the large-scale structure at z~0.3, consistent with coherent gas accretion onto filaments remaining efficient at higher M*. At z~0 we detect a transition toward perpendicular alignments at lower M*, while the gas and stellar components exhibit similar alignments relative to filaments, likely due to the cumulative effects of mergers and secular evolution.

</details>

Vinciguerra,M. von Wietersheim-Kramsta,N. A. Walton,A. H. Wright,H. W. Yeung,D. Scott*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用Euclid Q1强引力透镜数据，对95个椭圆星系在5 h⁻¹kpc孔径内的总质量进行测量，并标定其与光度、红移的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光度—总质量关系蕴含恒星形成效率与暗物质分布的信息，需要大样本、选择函数可控的观测标定，以检验星系结构与演化模型。

Method: 基于Euclid Quick Data Release 1的强引力透镜数据构建95个透镜样本，构建可复现的选择函数以校正选择效应；以5 h⁻¹kpc孔径内总质量M₅为观测量，拟合其随r波段光度与红移的标度关系。

Result: 在z=0.5、r波段光度10^10.7 L☉ h⁻²处，平均log₁₀(M₅ h M☉⁻¹)=11.30±0.02；M₅随光度标度指数β_L=0.71±0.07；固定光度下M₅随红移升高而下降。

Conclusion: 在并合生长与被动演化情景下，上述相关性意味着单个星系的M₅随时间增大；该测量为预测5 h⁻¹kpc尺度总质量分布的星系结构与演化模型提供了基准。

Abstract: Mass is among the most fundamental properties describing a galaxy. The relation between the luminosity and the total mass of a galaxy contains information on the efficiency of the star formation process and on the distribution of dark matter. We used strong gravitational lensing data from the Euclid Quick Data Release 1 (Q1) to constrain the total mass enclosed within an aperture of $5\,h^{-1}$kpc, $M_5$, of elliptical galaxies. Our analysis is based on a sample of 95 lenses, which we built by ensuring that their selection function is reproducible. This enabled us to correct our inference for selection effects. The average $\log_{10}{(M_5\,h\,M_\odot^{-1})}$ for a galaxy with an $r$-band luminosity of $10^{10.7}L_{\odot}\,h^{-2}$ at $z=0.5$ is $11.30\pm0.02$. The mass $M_5$ scales with luminosity to the power $β_\mathrm{L}=0.71\pm0.07$, and decreases with increasing redshift at fixed luminosity. Assuming a scenario in which galaxies grow by mergers and stellar populations evolve passively, these correlations imply that $M_5$ increases with time for individual galaxies. These measurements provide a useful benchmark for testing models of galaxy structure and evolution that make predictions on the distribution of total mass on the scales of $5\,h^{-1}$kpc.

</details>


### [84] [The L1157 filament as traced in HNCO by the LMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00336)
*A. I. Gómez-Ruiz,C. Codella,O. S. Rojas-García,R. Bachiller,D. Sánchez-Arguelles,M. Chavez Dagostino,G. Narayanan,A. Batta,V. Gómez-Rivera,I. Rodríguez-Montoya,E. E. Martínez-García,R. Naranjo-Romero*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用LMT对L1157外流蓝瓣进行HNCO成图，发现HNCO不仅示踪激波区还示踪约0.3 pc的原恒星诞生纤维结构，表明复杂化学前体在冷静态气体中已存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L1157外流是寻找复杂化学相关分子的原型目标；希望在大尺度（秒差距级）上成像关键生命前体分子，并验证LMT探测微弱、延展分子辐射的能力。

Method: 用LMT的SEQUOIA焦平面阵列观测HNCO的(4_0,4–3_0,3)跃迁（87.925 GHz），覆盖以主激波区L1157-B1为中心、3×3 arcmin²的区域，含原恒星区邻近大片气体。

Result: 探测到HNCO发射：既来自外流，也示踪一条长约0.3 pc的结构，即孕育该原恒星系统的纤维状云。

Conclusion: HNCO示踪分子云纤维的最致密部分；其在冷、静态纤维气体中的存在说明复杂化学的构筑单元在原恒星加热或激波之前已建立；观测也证明LMT适合绘制分子云中微弱延展成分。

Abstract: Context. The L1157 outflow is a prototype of chemically active outflows where to look for molecular species linked to complex chemistry. Aims. With the aim of imaging the large (parsec) scale structure of key prebiotic molecules, we have mapped the whole blue-lobe of the L1157 outflow with the Large Millimeter Telescope (LMT), proving the potential of this facility to detect weak and extended molecular emission. Methods. Isocyanic acid, HNCO, an important precursor of complex chemistry, was chosen to be observed in its (4_0,4-3_0-3) transition at 87.925 GHz with the SEQUOIA focal-plane array, covering an area of 3x3 arcmin2 centered at the position of the main shock L1157-B1, which included a large portion of the vicinity of the protostellar region. Results. The HNCO emission was detected not only from the outflow but it also was found tracing a structure of about 0.3 pc in length, corresponding to the filament from which the protostellar system was born. Conclusions. The HNCO molecule was found to be a tracer of the densest part of filaments in molecular clouds, and given its importance in the formation of more complex molecules, its presence in the cold, quiescent filamentary gas indicates that the building blocks of complex chemistry are already well established in stages prior to protostellar warming or shocks. By allowing the detection of structures related with the filament, these observations proved the LMT potentialities to map weak and extended components in molecular clouds.

</details>


### [85] [Modeling CO Line Luminosity with FIRE simulations: II. Comparison With Observations, CO Luminosity Functions and Redshift Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00368)
*Doğa Tolgay,J. Richard Bond,Norman W. Murray,Dongwoo Chung,Robert Feldmann,Andrew Wetzel*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 基于FIRE-2模拟并后处理SKIRT辐射转移与CLOUDY谱线发射，预测z=0–3星系CO(1-0)、CO(2-1)光度，与观测标度关系吻合，可用于CO强度映射建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CO分子谱线亮度是星系恒星形成与气体性质的重要示踪量，也是强度映射实验的关键信号；但观测在高红移与低金属丰度区间缺失或受灵敏度限制，需要可靠的物理模拟预测填补空白。

Method: 从FIRE-2宇宙学模拟中选取z=0、1、2、3的765、850、887、915个星系，用SKIRT做辐射转移，再用CLOUDY计算CO(1-0)与CO(2-1)线光度，并与现有观测对比（SFR–L_CO关系、弥散、L_CO–金属丰度斜率及红移依赖）。

Result: z=0时能再现大质量恒星形成星系与低金属丰度矮星系的SFR–L_CO(1-0)均值关系与弥散，并再现L_CO与金属丰度之间的超线性幂律斜率；z=1的CO(2-1)预测向低光度端弥散更宽，推断源于巡天灵敏度限制。固定SFR时L_CO(1-0)随红移每单位约下降一个量级，主要由金属丰度随红移下降驱动（同SFR同金属丰度时几乎无红移依赖）；固定恒星质量时预测光度无明显红移依赖。

Conclusion: 在被稳健探测的星系参数区间内，FIRE-2预测再现观测到的CO光度标度关系，增强了对无观测数据区域的预测信心，所给光度预测可用于构建CO强度映射实验的预测模型。

Abstract: We present predictions for the CO(1-0) and CO(2-1) luminosities of 765, 850, 887 and 915 galaxies at z=0, 1, 2 and 3, respectively, drawn from the Feedback in Realistic Environments (FIRE-2) simulations and post-processed with SKIRT radiative transfer and CLOUDY line emission calculations. We compare our CO line luminosity predictions with existing observations. At z=0, our predictions reproduce the observed scatter and mean relation between star formation rate (SFR) and CO(1-0) luminosity for both massive star-forming galaxies and low-metallicity dwarf galaxies. We also reproduce the observed superlinear power-law slope between CO(1-0) luminosity and metallicity. At z=1, our CO(2-1) predictions scatter to much lower luminosities than observed, which likely reflects the limited sensitivity of current surveys. At fixed SFR, our CO(1-0) luminosities decrease by roughly an order of magnitude per unit increase in redshift. This decline is likely driven by the decrease in metallicity with increasing redshift, since FIRE-2 galaxies with comparable SFR and metallicity produce similar CO(1-0) luminosities regardless of redshift. At fixed stellar mass, the predicted luminosities show no redshift dependence. These results show that our FIRE-2 predictions reproduce the observed scaling relations between CO(1-0) luminosity and galaxy properties wherever the observed galaxies are robustly detected. This gives us confidence in our predictions for the regime where no observational data exist, and suggests that our luminosity predictions can be used to build predictive models for CO line intensity mapping experiments.

</details>


### [86] [EMU/GAMA: Understanding the link between dust geometry and star formation rate in galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00741)
*U. T. Ahmed,A. M. Hopkins,B. Farley,M. Cowley,S. Phillipps,Y. A. Gordon,B. W. Holwerda,D. J. Bomans,K. A. Pimbblet,S. Casura,A. Battisti,D. A. Leahy,J. Prathap*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 利用EMU 887.5 MHz射电数据与GAMA G23场数据，研究尘埃遮蔽与SFR示踪量的关系，提出结合Balmer减缩与尘埃质量的光学厚尘埃几何模型参数H_dust，使Hα SFR估计与射电等传统示踪一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Balmer减缩（BD）是修正Hα消光的标准方法，但无法计入Hα无法逃逸的光学厚尘埃区，导致SFR_Hα被低估；需要一种能刻画光学厚尘埃程度的探针与修正方案。

Method: 将EMU Early Science 887.5 MHz射电连续谱数据与GAMA G23场光学数据交叉匹配；以射电光度与BD修正后的L_Hα之比（等价于SFR_radio/SFR_Hα）作为光学厚尘埃程度的探针；同时引入BD与尘埃质量M_d两类尘埃估计，分析其与L_radio/L_Hα及各星系属性的系统性依赖，并构建融合二者的尘埃几何参数H_dust。

Result: BD与M_d均与L_radio/L_Hα呈现系统性依赖关系，说明单一BD修正存在偏差；由BD与M_d组合的H_dust能更好地描述光学厚环境，基于其建立的SFR_Hα估计与常规SFR示踪量保持一致。

Conclusion: H_dust并非替代标准消光修正方法，而是对BD修正的物理补充，可表征传统方法忽略的光学厚尘埃区域，并给出与传统技术一致的SFR估计；射电/Hα比是约束尘埃几何的有效工具。

Abstract: We investigate the link between dust obscuration and star formation rate (SFR) estimators using Halpha luminosities, L_Halpha and radio luminosities, L_radio. While the Balmer Decrement (BD) is the standard tool for attenuating Halpha, it can fail to account for optically thick dust regions from which Halpha cannot escape. The ratio of SFRs estimated from radio luminosity and L_Halpha corrected for dust attenuation using the BD can be used as a probe of the degree of optically thick dust affecting Halpha. We explore this combining Evolutionary Map of the Universe (EMU) Early Science data at 887.5MHz in the Galaxy and Mass Assembly (GAMA) G23 field with the GAMA data. We draw on dust estimates using both the BD and the dust mass, M_d, demonstrating systematic underlying dependencies with L_radio/L_Halpha. By combining the BD and M_d, we quantify how they relate to L_radio/L_Halpha and other galaxy properties. We use this approach to establish an Halpha SFR (SFR_Halpha) estimator that accounts for optically thick dust, demonstrating its consistency with conventional SFR indicators. Rather than proposing a replacement for standard correction methods, we aim to demonstrate the effectiveness of a dust geometry model that integrates both BD and M_d parameters. This H_dust parameter provides a more physically complete representation of optically thick environments that traditional methods may overlook, and can produce SFR estimates consistent with traditional techniques.

</details>


### [87] [Introducing Aurora: A New Black Hole Seeding Model With Explicit Environmental Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01315)
*Harry George Chittenden,Darren Croton,Karl Glazebrook,Robert Adriel Mostoghiu Paun*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 提出Aurora黑洞种子模块：种子质量由所处环境连续调制，可在暗物质-only模拟中运行，并在z≃3.1对大质量晕中静止星系比例产生高达70%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期静止星系与"超质量"黑洞倾向于出现在致密环境中，而传统半解析模型采用固定种子质量，无法刻画这种环境依赖性，因此需要一个把种子质量与所在环境联系起来的机制。

Method: 在Mimic半解析框架内构建Aurora模块：每个新分辨晕的种子质量设为连续谱，耦合两个环境参数（局部超密度、周围晕的径向偏度）及成种时的晕质量；仅依赖晕质量与位置，故适用于纯暗物质模拟。在Millennium并合树上以Sage为基准进行标定，并通过打乱成种环境（保持质量谱不变）作对照检验。

Result: 该方案留下持久信号：大质量黑洞由众多前身晕的种子汇聚而成，z=0时种子贡献全部黑洞质量的29%；在致密、不对称环境中快速组装的星系出现"种子主导"的成长路径（区别于以吸积为主的多数星系）；z≃3.1时大质量晕中已播种星系的静止比例最高提升70%，过剩集中于超密度区、空隙区则出现亏缺，打乱环境后该梯度消失；两种环境耦合产生可分离的印记，主要拓宽星系—黑洞质量关系的弥散，同时保持恒星—晕质量关系及Sage其他汇总统计量。

Conclusion: 环境依赖成种是一种可检验的、具有持久效应的黑洞成种图像，可用于千兆秒差距尺度与更高分辨率的模拟，以理解当代深巡天中大质量黑洞系统的起源。

Abstract: We present Aurora, a black hole seeding module for the Mimic semi-analytic framework, in which the seed mass of each newly resolved halo is set by its environment. Motivated by evidence that early quiescent galaxies and overmassive black holes assemble in dense environments, Aurora replaces the fixed seed masses of conventional prescriptions with a continuous spectrum, coupling the seed mass to two environmental parameters, the local overdensity and the radial skewness of the surrounding haloes, and to the halo mass at seeding. Requiring only halo masses and positions, it applies to dark-matter-only simulations. Calibrated against Sage on the Millennium merger trees, this prescription leaves a lasting signal. Massive black holes are assembled from the seeds of many progenitors, seeds supplying 29 per cent of all black hole mass formed by $z=0$. A seed-dominated growth pathway, distinct from the accretion-dominated majority, emerges among galaxies assembled rapidly in dense, asymmetric environments. Environment-dependent seeding raises the quiescent fraction of seeded galaxies in massive haloes at $z \simeq 3.1$ by up to 70 per cent, with the excess concentrated in overdense environments, and a deficit in voids. Scrambling the seeding environments while preserving the mass spectrum erases this gradient. The two environmental couplings imprint separable signatures, broadening the scatter of the galaxy-black hole mass relation while preserving the stellar-halo mass relation and other Sage summary statistics. We discuss applying Aurora at gigaparsec scales and higher resolution, to understand the origins of massive black hole systems in contemporary deep surveys.

</details>


### [88] [Witnessing pre-processing at intermediate redshift: observational evidence of infalling groups onto a merging galaxy cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01344)
*D. Hernández-Lang,D. Pallero,A. Ivleva,E. Sextl,N. Muñoz-Elgueta,J. Pierel,A. Zenteno,M. Bayliss,A. Calamida,E. R. Carrasco,G. Mahler,R. Monteiro-Oliveira,P. H. Prado,A. Rest,A. Saro*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 结合MUSE积分场光谱与HST成像研究合并星系团SPT-CL J0307-6225（z=0.579）中两个候选下落群的5个高速星系，发现它们呈现星系间相互作用与持续恒星形成的空间分辨迹象，支持合并星系团环境中的预加工（pre-processing）情景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬灭星系比例随局部密度上升，其中一部分被认为发生在星系进入星系团之前的群环境中（预加工）。在合并星系团中，这一效应难以与星系团合并本身触发或抑制的恒星形成相区分，因此需要在合并星系团中识别候选下落群并检验其群尺度环境加工。

Method: 对SPT-CL J0307-6225中位于两个候选下落群的5个高速星系，联合MUSE积分场光谱与HST成像，反演[O II]运动学图、光谱分类与恒星形成率；并结合Magneticum模拟与相空间信息判断其是否成团、是否将被吸积。

Result: Group 1为束缚于星系团的形态受扰的相互作用对，至少一个成员为尘埃星暴；Group 2为致密三体系统，距星系团约4σ_v，其中两个星系共享共同的[O II]包层，主导成员为e(b)星暴并具有升高的电离气体速度弥散。两个星暴星系的SFR均落在场星系恒星形成主序的弥散范围内，符合适度相互作用触发暴发的预期；在Magneticum中约1%的同类质量星系处于Group 2的相空间位置，故该群束缚并非罕见。

Conclusion: 两个群均表现出空间分辨的星系—星系相互作用特征与持续恒星形成，支持合并星系团环境中的预加工情景；此类群尺度相互作用可能解释合并星系团中观测到的恒星形成增强。

Abstract: The fraction of quenched galaxies increases with local density, and part of this trend is thought to arise in groups before cluster accretion (pre-processing). In merging clusters, this is difficult to separate from star formation triggered or suppressed by the cluster merger itself. We investigate whether five high-velocity galaxies in the merging cluster SPT-CL~J0307-6225 ($z=0.579$), distributed in two candidate infalling groups, show signs of group-scale environmental processing prior to accretion. We combine MUSE integral-field spectroscopy and HST imaging to derive [\ion{O}{ii}] kinematic maps, spectral classes, and star-formation rates (SFR), to understand the properties of the galaxies. We further use the Magneticum simulations and the phase-space information to determine if these galaxies are part of two groups, and if they will be accreted onto the merging cluster. Group~1 is a morphologically disturbed interacting pair bound to the cluster, with at least one galaxy classified as dusty starburst. Group~2 is a compact triplet at $\sim$4$σ_v$ from the cluster, in which two galaxies share a common [\ion{O}{ii}] envelope and the dominant member, an e(b) starburst, shows an elevated ionised-gas velocity dispersion. The SFR of both starburst galaxies lie within the scatter of the field star-forming main sequence, as expected for modest, interaction-triggered bursts. Close pairs at the phase-space position of group~2 occur for $\sim$1\% of comparable-mass cluster galaxies in Magneticum, so it is not unlikely for the group to be bound. Both groups show spatially resolved signatures of galaxy--galaxy interactions alongside ongoing star formation, supporting a pre-processing scenario in the environment of a merging cluster. Such group-scale interactions may contribute to the enhanced star formation reported in merging clusters.

</details>


### [89] [Dynamically defined bulges reveal that early-type galaxies with compact bulges host more massive black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01469)
*Hanjing Shi,Ling Zhu,Sabine Thater,Yong Shi,Ana L. Chies-Santos,Luis Gabriel Dahmer Hahn,Zhengya Zheng*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文用动力学分解统一界定核球，重新考察早型星系的M_BH-M_bulge关系，发现核球致密度是第二参数，引入后散射降至约0.3 dex。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统M_BH-M_bulge关系依赖测光分解定义的核球，定义不统一且散射较大（约0.56 dex），且存在明显离群星系，需要用更物理、统一的方式界定核球并寻找第二参数。

Method: 从邻近、黑洞质量精确测量的星系中选取30个早型星系，使用宽场积分视场光谱数据（MUSE、ATLAS3D、PPAK）；对每个星系构建轨道叠加Schwarzschild模型，获得恒星轨道分布；统一将核球定义为圆度λ_z<0.7（替代定义λ_z<0.2）且r<3 kpc内的恒星轨道；由这些轨道重建核球空间密度分布，测量核球质量、尺寸R_bulge与面密度斜率γ_bulge。

Result: λ_z<0.7定义的动力学核球质量与测光核球质量系统一致；相比测光分解，散射由0.56降至0.47 dex。发现明显趋势：给定核球质量下，核球越致密（尺寸更小或密度斜率更陡）的星系黑洞越重；8个离群星系均为超大质量黑洞与超致密核球共存。

Conclusion: 结果被视为M_BH-M_bulge关系随星系并合历史演化的直接观测证据，该历史被记录在核球致密度中；引入M_BH-M_bulge-R_e,bulge（或γ_bulge）关系可将离群点纳入统一关系，并将整体散射降至约0.3 dex。

Abstract: We revisit the $M_{BH}-M_{bulge}$ relation for early-type galaxies by introducing a uniform bulge defined by dynamical decomposition. From the set of nearby galaxies with accurately measured black hole masses, we select 30 early-type galaxies that have been observed with wide-field integral-field unit instruments (MUSE, ATLAS3D, or PPAK). For each galaxy, we construct an orbit-superposition Schwarzschild model and obtain its stellar orbit distribution. In this framework, we define the bulge uniformly as comprising stellar orbits with circularity $λ_z < 0.7$ (or, in an alternative definition, $λ_z < 0.2$) and lying within $r < 3$ kpc. From these orbits, we reconstruct the spatial density distribution of the bulge and measure its mass ($M_{bulge}$), size ($R_{bulge}$) and surface density slope ($γ_{bulge}$). We find that the dynamical bulge defined by $λ_z < 0.7$ yields masses that are systematically consistent with the photometric bulge masses. Comparing to the bulge from photometric decomposition, using this dynamical bulge definition slightly decreases the scatter around the best-fitting from 0.56 to 0.47. More interestingly, we identify a clear trend: at a given bulge mass, galaxies hosting more compact bulges (i.e. smaller bulge sizes or steeper bulge density slopes) systematically contain more massive black holes; eight outliers show overmassive BHs together with ultra-compact bulges. We interpret this as direct observational evidence that the $M_{BH}-M_{bulge}$ relation evolves over a galaxy's merger history, with that history imprinted in bulge compactness. Incorporating compactness via an $M_{BH}-M_{bulge}-R_{e, bulge}$ (or $γ_{bulge}$) relation brings the outliers into agreement and reduces the overall scatter of the sample to $\sim 0.3$.

</details>

Abstract: The first few years of James Webb Space Telescope observations have identified previously undetected galaxies at $z>12$ and provided new constraints on galaxies at $z>8$. Many of these objects have stellar masses that are difficult to reconcile with $Λ$CDM, inferred from models not calibrated for the high-redshift Universe. This necessitates new, more astrophysically plausible stellar population models for estimating stellar masses of galaxies at cosmic dawn. When used in a configuration designed for high-redshift galaxies that requires young stellar population ages, the new models predict stellar masses that are ~0.3-0.5 dex lower than estimates from similar CIGALE analyses using models calibrated to the local Universe, and nearly 2 dex lower than estimates from analyses using other codes and softer constraints on stellar ages. Application of these physically motivated stellar populations suggests that stellar masses of early star-forming galaxies are not in tension with $Λ$CDM, and that galaxy formation from virialized halos remains a reasonable hypothesis at the highest redshifts. The SSPT SSPs and their CIGALE implementation are released publicly.

Abstract: We present a study of Coma-B122, an unusually clumpy star-forming ultra-diffuse galaxy (UDG) in the Coma cluster outskirts, using deep Keck Cosmic Web Imager integral-field spectroscopy and Subaru Hyper Suprime-Cam imaging. B122 hosts $\sim10$ luminous star-forming clumps contributing $\sim25$% of its luminosity and $\sim7$% of its stellar mass, with typical clump masses of $\sim10^6\ M_\odot$ comparable to the most massive super star clusters known in nearby starbursting dwarfs. The clumps form a distinctive ring configuration $\sim2$ kpc from the center. Full-spectrum fitting shows that $\sim12.5$% of the diffuse body's stellar mass formed during an extended episode of enhanced star formation over the past $\sim300$ Myr, within which all the clumps assembled. B122 shows evidence of weak ram-pressure stripping (RPS), including a diffuse light distribution truncated on the side facing the Coma center and extended to the opposite side, along with an asymmetric H I velocity profile. Its ordered stellar and ionized-gas rotation, cold gas fraction, and phase-space position are consistent with an early stage of infall. Spatial variations in clump star formation histories and gas-phase abundances further indicate that RPS-driven metal-poor gas inflow may have triggered and sustained the recent star formation. B122 may offer a rare present-day view of how UDGs assemble their globular cluster (GC) systems during cluster infall, although it remains uncertain if the clumps could survive as GCs. Future observations with higher spatial resolution and of more analogs are important to provide a complete picture of the formation and evolution of clumpy low-surface-brightness dwarf galaxies.

TL;DR: 基于JWST/NIRSpec对z≈6.5的104个[O III]发射体叠加静止系1200–2000 Å光谱，揭示窄线高电离发射、蓝移约200 km/s的强吸收（表明外流），并指出这些星系外流传播尺度仅3–9 pkpc，难以解释类星体光谱中重元素吸收体的频率，早期星系际介质富集或来自未被探测的低质量星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究z~6.5再电离末期星系的星际介质与星风性质（电离源、外流），并检验已知[O III]发射体能否解释类星体光谱中重元素吸收体的普遍存在，从而厘清早期星系际介质富集的来源。

Method: 对104个已知[O III]发射体（⟨z⟩=6.50，M_UV=-20.7，M_⋆=10^8.93 M_⊙）的JWST/NIRSpec光谱在静止系1200–2000 Å进行叠加分析；将叠加连续谱与低红移LBG复合谱比较；分析Lyα强度与速度偏移、O III]/C III]/He II等高电离发射线强度与线宽、发射线比值以判别电离源；测量高/低电离吸收线的蓝移；并用简单模型估算星风传播半径。

Result: 叠加连续谱与低红移LBG复合谱相似但存在差异；Lyα强度相当且相对[O III]系统速度红移，符合气体外流中的产生；O III]、C III]、He II略强于低红移样本但线宽很窄，不支持宽线AGN电离；线比略偏向硬恒星连续谱电离，但窄线核源贡献不能严格排除；多处强吸收线（高、低电离均有）蓝移~200 km/s，指示星风或携带金属的ISM外流；模型显示z~6.5多数恒星形成星系过于年轻，风速外流无法传播超过~3–9 pkpc。

Conclusion: 已探测的[O III]发射体群体本身不足以解释类星体光谱中重元素吸收体的出现频率；早期IGM富集很可能由聚集在这些较高质量系统周围、尚未被探测的低质量星系（M_⋆~10^5–10^6 M_⊙）主导，这些星系可能超出JWST/NIRCam WFSS探测极限。

Abstract: We present a stacked JWST/NIRSpec spectrum of 104 galaxies at $λ_{\rm rest}=1200\unicode{x2013}2000\;\mathrm{\mathring{A}}$ to study nebular emission and absorption from the interstellar medium (ISM) and stellar winds. The sample is drawn from known [O III] emitters with mean redshift $z=6.50$, absolute UV magnitude $M_\mathrm{UV}=-20.7$, and stellar mass $M_{\star}=10^{8.93}\;\mathrm{M_\odot}$. The stacked continuum closely resembles lower-redshift Lyman Break Galaxy composites, with a few notable differences. Ly$\;α$ has comparable strength and is redshifted relative to the [O III] systemic frame, consistent with production in gaseous outflows. High-ionization emission lines including O III], C III], and He II are slightly stronger than in lower-redshift stacks but exhibit narrow widths, disfavoring ionization from a broad-line AGN. Emission-line ratios slightly favor ionization by a hard stellar continuum, though a contribution from a narrow-line nuclear source cannot be strictly excluded. Numerous strong absorption lines at both high and low ionization are blueshifted by $\sim200\;\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$, again indicating outflows driven by stellar winds or entrained metal-enriched ISM. A simple model suggests that at $z\sim6.5$ most star-forming galaxies are too young for winds at these velocities to propagate beyond $\sim3\unicode{x2013}9$ proper kpc, implying that the observed [O III] emitter population alone cannot explain the frequency of heavy-element absorbers seen in quasar spectra. In this picture, early enrichment of the intergalactic medium likely arises from undetected low-mass galaxies ($M_\star\sim10^5\unicode{x2013}10^6\;\mathrm{M_\odot}$) clustered around the more massive systems detected in JWST/NIRCam WFSS surveys.

Result: 观测效应显著重塑云团统计：束斑模糊使云团更大、更圆、更弥散，噪声抹去暗弱云团并剥离低密度气体；加之与前景区面发射的混淆，超过80%的云团（平静期可达95%）无法探测；被探测者显得质量更小、湍流更弱、更不拉长，柱密度被低估约2倍，仅速度可靠恢复。校正后模拟云团大体再现GBT样本的性质与天区分布，但缺少速度>250 km/s、纬度b>10°的云团；模拟UV吸收显示的暖电离气体柱密度处于观测低端，b>10°处X射线发射测度偏低。

Conclusion: 真实银心云团群体应比观测到的数量更多、质量更大、湍流更强、更拉长；要完全再现观测，可能需要更强的近期星暴或额外反馈机制。

Abstract: High-velocity neutral hydrogen (HI) clouds found up to several kiloparsecs above the Galactic centre (GC) provide some of the most direct evidence for a nuclear wind in the Milky Way, yet the properties inferred for these clouds are influenced by how they are observed. Starting from a magnetohydrodynamical simulation in which supernova feedback drives a multiphase nuclear outflow, we generate mock HI observations matched to the angular resolution and sensitivity of Green Bank Telescope (GBT) surveys of the GC and analyse them in the same way as the real data. We find that observational effects substantially reshape the cloud population: beam smearing makes clouds appear larger, rounder and more diffuse, while noise erases faint clouds and strips low-density gas from the others. These effects combined, together with confusion with foreground disk emission, prevent the detection of more than 80% of the clouds (up to 95% during more quiescent phases), while those detected appear less massive, less turbulent and less elongated, with column densities underestimated by a factor of two; only their velocities are reliably recovered. Once these effects are included, the simulated clouds broadly reproduce the properties and sky distribution of the GBT population, but lack the fastest (> 250 km/s ) and highest-latitude (b > 10° ) clouds. Mock UV absorption and soft X-ray observations support this picture, with warm-ionized gas column densities at the low end of the observed range and X-ray emission measures too low at b > 10° . Our results imply that the true GC cloud population is more numerous, massive, turbulent and elongated than observed, and that fully reproducing observations may require a stronger recent starburst or additional feedback.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银心化学丰度测量受高消光、星场拥挤和距离限制，通常只能依赖近红外波段最亮的AGB星和M型巨星。被Sgr A*以Hills机制抛射的超高速星将其原生化学成分带到银晕等易观测区域，是探测银心化学的独特探针。

Conclusion: 综合化学、运动学及核星团拥有大量年老富金属星的特点，倾向于核星团起源。与WINERED-HVS1及S0-6/S10丰度指向不同母体相比，暗示S星团存在多个母体族群。DESI-312是光学波段利用被抛射矮星采样银心化学的示范案例，可补充近红外巨星研究。

Abstract: Chemical abundances in the Galactic Centre (GC) are challenging to measure because of high extinction, crowding, and distance, and are typically limited to the brightest AGB and M giants observed in the near-infrared. In contrast, hypervelocity stars (HVSs) ejected from the GC by Sgr A* carry their natal composition into accessible regions such as the Milky Way halo, making them unique probes of GC chemistry. We present high-resolution optical Magellan/MIKE spectroscopy of DESI-312, a bound HVS candidate in the halo with super-solar metallicity ($[\mathrm{Fe/H}]=0.2\pm0.1$). This sets it apart from accreted halo populations on radial orbits and is most naturally explained by GC formation and Hills-mechanism ejection. Updated isochrone fits give an age of 7.4-11.3 Gyr; together with its metallicity and kinematics, this strengthens the case for a GC origin. Korg and Turbospectrum spectral synthesis yields $[\mathrm{X/Fe}]$ for 22 elements, plus Li from an independent line-by-line NLTE analysis. As DESI-312 is near the main sequence, its C, N, and O abundances are unaltered by dredge-up; to our knowledge, these are the first such measurements of unevolved GC material. Its abundance pattern lies where the nuclear star cluster (NSC), nuclear stellar disc (NSD), and inner-bulge sequences overlap, with a Na enhancement ($[\mathrm{Na/Fe}]=+0.16^{+0.03}_{-0.04}$) more modest than in NSC giants of similar metallicity. Chemistry alone thus cannot identify its progenitor; we nevertheless favour the NSC, given its large reservoir of old, metal-rich stars and the star's kinematics. Alongside WINERED-HVS1 and the S-star S0-6/S10, whose abundances point to different progenitors, this tentatively suggests multiple progenitor populations for the S-star cluster. DESI-312 is a test case for sampling GC chemistry with ejected dwarfs at optical wavelengths, complementing near-infrared studies of giants.

</details>

*Yimeng Tang,Yifei Luo,Conghao Zhou*
