### [110] [Beyond Point Photo-$z$ Estimates: Normalizing Flows for Tomographic $N(z)$ Reconstruction with DESI DR1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00121)
*Zhuo Chen,Antoine Rocher,Kwan Chuen Chan,Jean-Paul Kneib*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文比较条件归一化流（NF）与随机森林（RF）、分位数随机森林（QRF）在弱引力透镜层析红移分布N(z)重建中的表现，基于DESI DR1光谱与Legacy Surveys DR10测光数据。NF在点估计和高红移层析N(z)重建上更优，经事后PDF校准后平均红移偏差可降至极低水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确标定层析红移分布N(z)是弱引力透镜宇宙学分析的关键前提，需要评估能提供后验PDF的机器学习方法（尤其是生成式模型）相对传统树模型在点估计与分布层面上的优劣。

Method: 使用DESI DR1光谱红移（0<z_spec<4.5）与Legacy Surveys DR10测光，训练集110万源、测试集200万源且天区不重叠；对比条件归一化流（NF）与随机森林、分位数随机森林。评价包括点估计三项指标（散布、离群率等）及分布层面指标（Wasserstein距离、JS散度、概率重叠度），并对NF后验PDF堆叠得到层析N(z)；此外用留出验证样本进行红移依赖的事后PDF校准。

Result: NF在0<z_spec<1.6区间点估计散布与离群率最低，且在z_spec>1.6时三项指标全面优于两个基线。堆叠NF后验PDF的层析N(z)重建优于QRF，高红移尤甚：z_median>2.5时NF平均红移偏差为0.060±0.031（QRF为0.318），概率重叠0.905±0.008（QRF为0.787）。校准后0.2<z_median<1.0四个bin的平均红移偏差绝对值在0.0016–0.0019之间，低于基准0.002(1+z)；最高红移bin偏差降至0.016±0.011。

Conclusion: 条件归一化流提供的后验PDF在层析N(z)重建上具有明显潜力，尤其在高红移；但模型在不同星系族群间迁移时仍存在挑战。

Abstract: Accurate calibration of tomographic redshift distributions, $N(z)$ is essential for cosmological analysis of weak lensing data. We investigate conditional normalizing flows (NFs) and compare their performance with random forest (RF) and quantile random forest (QRF) models. We consider DESI galaxy and quasar targets from DESI DR1 spectroscopic data and with the photometry from Legacy Surveys DR10. We train on 1.1 million sources and test on 2.0 million sources in a non-overlapping sky region ($0<z_{\rm spec}<4.5$). For point estimation, the NF achieves the lowest scatter and outlier fraction at $0<z_{\rm spec}<1.6$ and outperforms both baselines across all three metrics at $z_{\rm spec}>1.6$. By introducing distribution-level metrics like Wasserstein distance and JS divergence, we find that stacking NF posterior PDFs provides more accurate tomographic $N(z)$ reconstruction than QRF, particularly at high redshifts. For $z_{\rm median}>2.5$, the NF achieves a smaller absolute mean-redshift bias ($0.060\pm0.031$ versus $0.318$) and higher probability overlap ($0.905\pm0.008$ versus $0.787$) than QRF. We apply redshift-dependent post-hoc PDF calibration derived from a held-out validation sample. After calibration, the absolute mean-redshift biases in four bins spanning $0.2<z_{\rm median}<1.0$ range from $0.0016$ to $0.0019$, with central values below referring benchmark $0.002(1+z)$. In the highest-redshift bin, calibration further reduces the bias to $0.016\pm0.011$. Our results demonstrate the potential of NF PDFs for tomographic $N(z)$ reconstruction while highlighting challenges in transferring models across galaxy populations.

</details>


### [111] [A multiprobe $Λ$CDM consistency analysis with gravitational-wave standard sirens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00415)
*T. M. C. Abbott,M. Aguena,F. Andrade-Oliveira,S. Avila,D. Bacon,D. Brooks,H. Camacho,R. Camilleri,G. Campailla,A. Carnero Rosell,J. Carretero,R. Cawthon,C. Chang,L. N. da Costa,M. E. da Silva Pereira,S. Desai,H. T. Diehl,P. Doel,A. Ferté,D. L. Hollowood,K. Honscheid,D. Huterer,D. Laghi,S. Lee,S. MacBride,J. L. Marshall,I. McMahon,J. Mena-Fernández,R. Miquel,J. Muir,J. Myles,R. L. C. Ogando,A. A. Plazas Malagón,J. Prat,M. Rodríguez-Monroy,R. Rosenfeld,S. Samuroff,E. Sanchez,D. Sanchez-Cid,I. Sevilla-Noarbe,M. Soares-Santos,E. Suchyta,M. E. C. Swanson,V. Vikram,N. Weaverdyck,J. Weller,M. Yamamoto*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 首次将LVK GWTC-5.0引力波标准汽笛与DES Y6 3×2pt、BAO、SNe Ia、DESI BAO及Planck/ACT/SPT CMB联合，在同构先验下用normalizing flows进行多探针ΛCDM张力评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波目录规模增长使多信使宇宙学成为独立检验ΛCDM的途径，需要首次把标准汽笛纳入多探针ΛCDM张力（S8、H0）评估。

Method: 联合LVK GWTC-5.0标准汽笛、DES Y6弱透镜+成团3×2pt、BAO、SNe Ia、DESI BAO，与Planck、ACT、SPT主CMB数据对比；所有探针采用同构先验，通过normalizing flows进行联合，量化S8差异与H0张力。

Result: 标准汽笛加入3×2pt使Ωm–S8平面约束改善约30%，H0约束至6.2%；低红移探针（含标准汽笛）给出S8=0.793±0.010、Ωm=0.308(+0.007/-0.006)、H0=69.4(+4.1/-4.4) km/s/Mpc；但当前GW灵敏度下S8差异与H0张力基本不变。

Conclusion: 该工作给出首个含标准汽笛的ΛCDM张力评估，确立标准汽笛为成熟的宇宙学探针，可与LSST、Euclid、Roman等巡天数据平等联合。

Abstract: Multi-messenger cosmology offers an independent path to stress-test the $Λ$CDM model as gravitational wave (GW) catalogs grow in size. We present the first multiprobe assessment of $Λ$CDM tensions incorporating GW standard sirens: LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA Collaboration (LVK) fifth Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog (GWTC-5.0) standard sirens are combined with Dark Energy Survey (DES) Year 6 weak lensing and galaxy clustering (3$\times$2pt), baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO), and type Ia supernovae (SNe Ia), together with Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) BAO, and confronted with primary cosmic microwave background (CMB) measurements from Planck, the Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT), and the South Pole Telescope (SPT). All probes are analyzed under homogeneous priors and combined via normalizing flows, and we quantify the $S_8$ discrepancy and $H_0$ tension with respect to CMB. Adding standard sirens to the 3$\times$2pt analysis improves the constraints in the $Ω_m$-$S_8$ plane by $\sim$30%, while constraining $H_0$ to 6.2%. We present cosmological constraints from the low-redshift probes including standard sirens, yielding $S_8 = 0.793 \pm 0.010$, $Ω_m = 0.308^{+0.007}_{-0.006}$, and $H_0 = 69.4^{+4.1}_{-4.4}$ km s$^{-1}$ Mpc$^{-1}$. At current GW sensitivity, however, both the $S_8$ discrepancy and the $H_0$ tension remain essentially unchanged. Our analysis nonetheless delivers the first assessment of $Λ$CDM tensions computed with standard sirens included among the probes, and establishes them as a mature cosmological probe -- ready to be combined on equal footing with established datasets in forthcoming surveys such as the Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST), Euclid, and Roman.

</details>


### [112] [How tightly can PBH abundance information constrain scalar-induced gravitational waves?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00873)
*Boliang Yu,Ruixin Zhou,Zi-Yu Tang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 在固定QCD形成方案下，两组平滑对称对数正态谱可满足相同的PBH丰度条件（有限恒星质量区间内的精确丰度与总丰度约束），但给出不成比例的高阶SIGW谱：低频nanohertz窗口平均差异约20%，高频窗口差异近60倍，说明PBH与SIGW联合推断不能以单一模板对应单次丰度拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBH丰度与标量诱导引力波（SIGW）以不同物理权重探测同一原初扰动，实践中却常以单一张量模板匹配单次丰度拟合。作者希望量化"精确丰度信息"对原初谱形状的预测能力边界，检验丰度约束能否唯一确定SIGW谱。

Method: 对高斯扰动、固定QCD形成方案，构造两个平滑对称的对数正态标量谱，使其同时满足同一有限恒星质量区间的精确绝对PBH丰度、精确总丰度以及外部种群上限约束；比较二者的领头阶SIGW谱在两个相邻nanohertz频率窗口的响应，并分析峰位—宽度—幅度的补偿机制与模耦合来源。另考察以QCD及随机峰曲率形成方案下的局部有限宽度成分，以及将质量信息扩展到种群大部分丰度范围（更大质量覆盖）的影响。

Result: 两谱在低窗口平均差异约20%，在高频窗口差异近60倍，无法用单一重标度表示。机理：丰度计算中峰位与宽度同幅度相互补偿；高频窗口的能量几乎全部来自两个波数均高于原初谱峰的标量模对。主丰度基准下，局部有限宽度成分在固定nanohertz频率处因不同模耦合产生更大对比度（两种形成方案均如此）。对独立生成的种群，扩大质量信息覆盖会限制对数正态峰位—宽度的补偿并减小高频窗口对比，但两窗口响应不等仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 丰度信息不足以唯一确定原初谱形状，因而不足以唯一确定SIGW背景。PBH–SIGW联合推断必须传播与PBH丰度信息相符的原初谱形状集合（而非将单一张量模板赋给单次丰度拟合），并应区分不同频率窗口的独立响应。

Abstract: Primordial black hole (PBH) abundances and scalar-induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) probe the same primordial fluctuations through different physical weights. We examine the predictive power of precise synthetic information on absolute PBH abundances over a finite stellar-mass interval, together with an exact total abundance and external population bounds. For Gaussian perturbations under a fixed quantum chromodynamics (QCD) formation prescription, two smooth, symmetric lognormal spectra satisfy the same abundance conditions while producing nonproportional leading-order SIGW spectra. The two spectra differ by about 20% when averaged over the lower of two adjacent nanohertz windows but by nearly a factor of 60 in the higher-frequency window, so the difference cannot be represented by a single rescaling of the gravitational-wave background. Peak position and width compensate with amplitude in the abundance calculation, while the higher-frequency window draws almost all its energy from scalar-mode pairs with both wavenumbers above the primordial peak. Under the principal abundance benchmark, local finite-width components yield larger contrasts at a fixed nanohertz frequency through distinct mode couplings under both the QCD and stochastic peak-curvature formation prescriptions. For a separately generated population, extending mass information over most of its abundance restricts the lognormal peak-position and width compensation and reduces the higher-window contrast, while the unequal window responses persist. These results show why joint PBH-SIGW inference must propagate primordial shapes compatible with the PBH abundance information rather than assign one tensor template to a single abundance fit.

</details>


### [113] [Cuscuton Fifth Force: Implications of Non-Dynamical Scalars for Structure Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01228)
*Adam Smith,Ilaria Andrei,Maria Mylova,Niayesh Afshordi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 研究Jordan frame中物质最小耦合的有效cuscuton宇宙学，推导背景与线性扰动方程，发现虽无传播自由度但代数响应介导第五力，并用改造版CLASS计算CMB与物质功率谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cuscuton作为服从约束而非演化方程的标量场，可在不引入传播标量自由度的情况下修改引力。作者希望系统考察物质在Jordan frame最小耦合（从而在Einstein frame与cuscuton共形耦合）时的宇宙学后果，厘清第五力的来源、强度与符号。

Method: 在Jordan frame中令物质最小耦合，转换到Einstein frame后物质与cuscuton共形耦合；推导背景与线性扰动方程，分析cuscuton的代数响应如何介导第五力，以及共形因子对Einstein frame粒子质量和物质扰动摩擦项的修正；构造含tracking解与变号解的幂律模型族；用修改版CLASS（含复合时期粒子质量依赖）计算基准模型的CMB温度谱与线性物质功率谱，不进行数据拟合。

Result: 尽管cuscuton本身不传播，其代数响应仍介导第五力，且该力强度与符号由背景演化决定而非独立耦合常数；共形因子同时改变粒子质量与扰动摩擦。最强基准中，第五力使亚视界有效引力耦合改变约1%，小于物质功率谱与声学尺度的相应变化；变号解中该修正在大部宇宙历史为负，即非传播场可削弱而非增强引力吸引。

Conclusion: 有效cuscuton理论在无传播标量自由度下仍能通过共形耦合产生可观测的第五力效应，其效应与背景演化紧密关联，并在变号解中表现为引力减弱；这一机制为区分cuscuton类修改引力模型提供了可检验的宇宙学信号。

Abstract: The cuscuton is a scalar field that obeys a constraint rather than an evolution equation, so it modifies gravity without adding a propagating scalar degree of freedom. We study the cosmology of effective cuscuton theory when matter is minimally coupled in the Jordan frame, and therefore conformally coupled to the cuscuton in the Einstein-frame. We derive the background and linear perturbation equations and show that the algebraic cuscuton response mediates a fifth force even though the scalar itself does not propagate. The same conformal factor also changes Einstein-frame particle masses and the friction of matter perturbations, tying the strength and sign of the force to the background evolution rather than to an independent coupling. We study a power-law family of models which contain both tracking solutions and sign-switching solutions; in the latter the effective cuscuton density runs from negative during matter domination to a positive, cosmological-constant-like value today. Using a modified version of CLASS, including the induced particle-mass dependence of recombination, we illustrate the corresponding changes to CMB temperature and linear matter power spectra for benchmark models, without fitting to data. In the strongest benchmarks the fifth force changes the effective gravitational coupling on sub-horizon scales by about one percent, smaller than the accompanying changes to the matter power spectrum and the acoustic scale. For the sign-switching solutions this change is negative over most of cosmic history, so a non-propagating field can weaken rather than strengthen gravitational attraction.

</details>


### [114] [Bayesian and frequentist constraints on dark energy parameterizations using non-CMB data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01505)
*Shubham Barua,Ansh Chandrakar,Shantanu Desai*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Comparing four dark energy parameterizations (TQD, AT, CPL, GEDE) against ΛCDM using non-CMB data (DES-Dovekie SNe, DESI BAO DR2, GRBs, RSD) in Bayesian and frequentist frameworks, the authors find model preference is highly contingent on the parameterization, dataset and metric: only CPL shows phantom crossing, and TQD is the only extended model favored by all metrics once SNe are included.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI BAO + CMB data hint at dynamical dark energy with phantom crossing in w0-wa fits, but BAO and CMB are in tension within ΛCDM. To avoid this tension, the authors test whether evidence for DDE persists using purely non-CMB datasets, and whether conclusions depend on the choice of parameterization and statistical metric.

Method: Binned BAO and SNe constraints combined with GRB correlations and RSD (AP/RSD); grids/ensembles for first-order thawing solutions; Gaussian likelihoods; Bayesian and frequentist comparison.

Result: GRBs contribute only marginally, SNe tighten constraints considerably. CPL shows phantom crossing at z~0.5; TQD and AT improve best-fit χ² without phantom crossing. With SNe: TQD moderately favored by Bayes factor and positively by AIC/DIC; CPL and AT positively favored by AIC/DIC but Bayes factor weakly prefers ΛCDM over CPL and is inconclusive for AT (Occam penalty from prior volume). GEDE disfavored by all three metrics.

Conclusion: The preference for dynamical dark energy is not robust: it depends on the parameterization, the dataset combination, and the comparison metric used. Only the thawing quintessence parameterization receives consistent (if moderate) support across metrics once SNe are included; CPL's apparent phantom crossing should be interpreted with caution.

Abstract: Data from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) BAO combined with the cosmic microwave background (CMB) have provided evidence for dynamical dark energy (DDE) with a phantom crossing ($w(z) < -1$) when constraining $w_0-w_a$ parameterizations. Given the tension between BAO and CMB data within $Λ$CDM, we use only non-CMB data: Type Ia Supernovae (SNe) from the DES-Dovekie compilation, DESI BAO DR2, Gamma-Ray Bursts (GRBs) and Redshift-Space-Distortion (RSD) measurements. We use the dataset combinations GRB$+$BAO, BAO$+$SNe and GRB$+$BAO$+$SNe, each with RSD. We perform both Bayesian and frequentist analyses of two quintessence-inspired thawing dark energy parameterizations: the thawing quintessence parameterization (TQD) and the Algebraic Thawing parameterization (AT), together with the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) and the Generalized Emergent Dark Energy parameterizations (GEDE). The GRB sample, used in this work, contributes only marginally to the constraints, while SNe tighten them considerably. The CPL equation of state shows a phantom crossing at $z\sim 0.5$, while TQD and AT improve the best-fit $χ^2$ over $Λ$CDM, without showing phantom crossing. When SNe are included, TQD is preferred by the Bayes factor (moderately), AIC and DIC (positively). For CPL and AT, AIC and DIC prefer the extended model positively, while the Bayes factor weakly prefers $Λ$CDM over CPL and is inconclusive for AT. This difference is consistent with the Occam penalty from the prior volume in the Bayes factor. GEDE, with the DE density restricted to be suppressed at high redshifts is disfavored by all three metrics. Hence, the preference for DDE depends on the parameterization, the dataset and the comparison metric used.

</details>


### [115] [foraois: Fast, Flexible Extended Press-Schechter Merger Trees with a General First-Crossing Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01580)
*C. Power,S. Balu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: foraois is an open-source Python framework that generates Monte Carlo dark matter halo merger trees, decoupling the linear density field, halo collapse physics, and tree-construction algorithms so that different cosmologies, collapse prescriptions, and sampling methods can be combined modularly, validated against extensive tests and coupled to a semi-analytic galaxy formation model.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Merger-tree generation in the excursion-set picture typically entangles cosmological input, collapse physics, and algorithmic sampling in monolithic codes, hindering comparison of prescriptions and reuse across CDM/WDM/FDM cosmologies and constrained (Brownian-bridge) histories. A modular, open framework is needed.

Method: The framework separates three layers: (1) linear density field — linear matter power spectra for CDM, WDM, and FDM; (2) collapse physics — a modular barrier treatment with extension points for alternative barrier models; (3) algorithmics — two solvers for progenitor-mass sampling: the semi-empirical Parkinson, Cole & Helly (2008) algorithm (fitted correction to the EPS progenitor distribution) and the numerical solution of the Markovian first-crossing equation of Zhang & Hui (2006), which under the default real-space top-hat window approximates the correlated-walk problem. Constrained merger histories via the Nadler et al. (2023) Brownian-bridge construction are also supported.

Result: Extensive validation and scaling tests are performed, and an illustrative coupling of foraois to a lightweight semi-analytical galaxy formation model is demonstrated.

Conclusion: foraois provides a reusable, extensible, open-source platform for Monte Carlo merger-tree construction, enabling systematic comparison of collapse prescriptions, dark matter models, and tree-building algorithms within one common framework.

Abstract: We present foraois (Gaelic for Forest; FAW-reesh), an open-source Python framework for generating Monte Carlo dark matter halo merger tree histories. foraois separates the description of the linear density field, the physics of halo collapse, and the algorithmic construction of halo merger histories, allowing different cosmologies, collapse prescriptions, and tree building approaches to be combined within a common framework. The package implements two approaches to the same merger-tree problem of sampling progenitor masses in the excursion-set picture: the semi-empirical algorithm of Parkinson, Cole, & Helly (2008), which applies a fitted correction to the EPS progenitor distribution, and the numerical solution of the Markovian first-crossing equation developed by Zhang & Hui (2006), which for the default real-space top-hat window is an approximation to the correlated-walk problem. Linear matter power spectra for Cold, Warm, and Fuzzy Dark Matter (CDM, WDM, and FDM) can be supplied, while the modular collapse treatment provides an extension point for alternative barrier models. The framework also supports constrained merger histories based on the Brownian-bridge construction of Nadler et al. (2023). We run extensive validation and scaling tests, and also show an illustrative example of coupling foraois to a light-weight semi-analytical model of galaxy formation.

</details>


### [116] [The nucleon is not a hard sphere: screening fifth forces with Skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02213)
*Philippe Brax,Clare Burrage,Adam Moss*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文指出实验室第五力（chameleon、symmetron）搜寻中把核子当作均匀球体处理是不准确的，改用具有常密度核心与 r^-6 介子尾（及其在物理π介子质量处的指数截断）的核子轮廓，推导了相应的解析屏蔽电荷，并发现该修正系统性扩展了强耦合区域的排除界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室探测chameleon和symmetron第五力时以单个核子作为源或探针，但通常将核子模型化为均匀球体。受Skyrme图像启发，核子更真实地具有常密度核心与稀薄的外围介子尾，这种外部稀释结构可能显著影响屏蔽电荷的计算，从而影响对耦合强度的约束。

Method: 构建含常密度核心与r^-6介子尾的核子密度轮廓，并考虑其在物理π介子质量处的指数截断；对均匀球体与r^-6轮廓分别推导解析屏蔽电荷，指数截断情形则通过数值求解进行比较；随后将差异代入已发表的实验分析中重新评估排除区域。

Result: 由于外部尾部过于稀薄，在任何耦合强度下都无法被屏蔽，r^-6尾部在强屏蔽下相对球体电荷产生幂律增强，指数截断尾部则给出较弱的对数增强。将该差异传播到已发表分析后，核子源排除区域系统性向强耦合区延伸：μ=1 keV下中子干涉术的symmetron约束所达最大自耦合比均匀球中子高约1.4个数量级（手征极限下3.0个数量级）；在暗能量能标下，氢1s–2s的chameleon界限比均匀球质子弱约1.8个数量级（手征极限下2.4个数量级），并超过μ子素界限。

Conclusion: 核子的空间轮廓（尤其是稀薄、不可屏蔽的介子尾）是第五力实验约束中不可忽略的物理因素；采用更真实的r^-6型核子轮廓会系统性改变强耦合区的排除界限，甚至改变不同实验间的相对强弱排序，故未来分析应摒弃均匀球近似。

Abstract: Laboratory searches for chameleon and symmetron fifth forces increasingly rely on individual nucleons as sources or probes, but model them as uniform spheres. Motivated by the Skyrme picture, we instead consider a nucleon profile with a constant-density core and an $r^{-6}$ pion tail, together with its exponential truncation at the physical pion mass. We derive analytic screening charges for the uniform sphere and the $r^{-6}$ profile, and extend the comparison to the exponential truncation at the physical pion mass using numerical solutions. Because the outer tail is too dilute to be screened at any coupling strength, the $r^{-6}$ tail produces a power-law enhancement over the sphere charge in strong screening, and the exponential tail has a weaker, logarithmic enhancement. Propagating this difference through the published analyses, nucleon-sourced exclusion regions extend systematically further into the strongly coupled regime. For example, symmetron constraints from neutron interferometry at $μ=1\,\mathrm{keV}$ reach maximal self-couplings about $1.4$ orders of magnitude higher than for a uniform-sphere neutron ($3.0$ orders of magnitude in the chiral limit),and at the dark-energy scale the hydrogen $1s$--$2s$ chameleon bound reaches couplings $1.8$ orders of magnitude weaker than for a uniform-sphere proton ($2.4$ in the chiral limit), overtaking the muonium bound.

</details>


### [117] [Thirty years of primordial deuterium abundances: A provenance-aware statistical reanalysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02289)
*Richard de Grijs,Giuseppe Bono*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 作者构建了1994–2026年间247篇文献、957个原初D/H值的前溯（provenance-aware）数据库，发现已发表数据高度重复依赖：有效独立吸收体仅约16.28个，但D/H≈2.5×10⁻⁵的中心值仍稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初氘丰度是检验大爆炸核合成（BBN）最精确的观测手段之一，但既有文献建立在数量偏少的高红移吸收系统之上；发表数量密度可能高估了统计独立性，需量化其数据来源与复用结构。

Method: 构建涵盖1994–2026年、247篇文献、957个已发表D/H值的溯源数据库，尽量将观测条目对应到其物理吸收体；统计条目类型构成、吸收体复用率，并以逆赫芬达尔—赫希曼指数（inverse HHI）度量有效独立表示数；同时进行吸收体层面的替代性统计处理与剔除主导系统的稳健性检验。

Result: 795个观测条目中80.1%为重复使用的个体值或汇编值，仅11.4%为对单个吸收系统的新测定；共识别52个不同吸收体，但出现最频繁的8个系统占可识别吸收体提及的62.0%；全库有效表示仅相当于16.28个等权吸收体。现代共识值10⁵(D/H)≈2.5对吸收体层面的替代处理稳健，且剔除8个主导系统后仍成立；但文献级汇编的“表观精度”对重复表示明显更敏感。此外发现约2013–2014年汇编文献出现同步转折，分析趋于选择性与同质性，而较旧的个体值仍在流通。

Conclusion: 现代D/H共识在中心值上稳健，但其底层文献的统计独立性远低于发表密度所暗示的水平；精度宇宙学中应重视溯源感知分析，以区分真实观测增益与数据复用带来的表观精度。

Abstract: (ABRIDGED:) Primordial deuterium provides one of the most precise observational tests of Big Bang nucleosynthesis, yet the published literature is built on a comparatively small number of high-redshift absorption systems. We construct a provenance-aware database of 957 published primordial-D/H values from 247 publications spanning 1994--2026 and trace observational entries, wherever possible, to their underlying physical absorbers. Of the 795 observational entries, 80.1 percent represent reused individual or compilation values; only 11.4 percent correspond to new determinations of individual absorption systems. Although 52 distinct absorbers are identified overall, the eight most frequently represented systems account for 62.0 percent of all identifiable absorber mentions. An inverse Herfindahl--Hirschman measure applied to our full database gives an effective representation equivalent to only 16.28 equally represented absorbers, demonstrating that the published literature is strongly concentrated around a much smaller effective subset. Despite this strong internal dependence, the modern consensus value of $10^5({\rm D/H})\simeq2.5$ is robust to alternative absorber-level treatments and survives removal of the eight dominant systems. However, the apparent precision of literature-level compilations is more sensitive to repeated representation than a simple count of published values implies. We also identify a synchronised transition in the compilation literature around 2013--2014, associated with increasingly selective and homogeneous analyses while older individual values continued to circulate. The modern D/H consensus is therefore robust in central value, but the underlying literature is less statistically independent than its publication density suggests. This thus highlights the importance of provenance-aware analysis in precision cosmology.

</details>


### [118] [Recovering Missing Degrees of Freedom in Simulation-Trained Field-Level Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02627)
*Masato Shirasaki,Shiro Ikeda*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文将「方差收缩」解释为生成模型缺失有效自由度的诊断信号，并对连续归一化流的生成动力学引入物理启发的潜变量修正，在不增加训练模拟的情况下恢复部分缺失变异性，提升弱引力透镜场级推断的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟训练的生成模型已成为宇宙学场级推断的重要工具，尤其适用于强非线性区域。但训练集有限导致方差收缩，即学习到的生成动力学未能刻画底层物理过程在实现层面的部分变异性，这会削弱推断的可靠性与稳健性。

Method: 将方差收缩视为缺失有效自由度的诊断指标；在连续归一化流（continuous normalizing flow）的生成动力学中引入物理启发的潜变量修正（physics-informed latent modifications），从而在无需额外训练模拟的条件下补足缺失的变异性；以弱引力透镜场级推断为代表性应用，并通过后验分析考察潜变量方向在推断中是否被有效利用。

Result: 潜变量修正恢复了相当比例的缺失变异性，且不依赖新增训练模拟；后验分析表明学习到的潜变量方向在推断中被主动利用，表现出类似有效冗余参数（nuisance parameters）的行为，吸收训练分布与目标分布之间的部分失配；模型对分布外偏移（out-of-distribution shifts）的稳健性得到改善。

Conclusion: 方差收缩可作为识别模拟训练生成模型中缺失自由度的实用诊断工具，而通过物理启发的生成动力学修正是恢复这些自由度的可行框架，为宇宙学及其他非线性场级推断任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Simulation-trained generative models are emerging as a powerful framework for field-level inference in cosmology, particularly in strongly nonlinear regimes where analytical approaches become intractable. However, the finite size of training datasets often leads to variance shrinkage, indicating that part of the realization-level variability of the underlying physical process is not captured by the learned generative dynamics. In this Letter, we interpret variance shrinkage as a diagnostic of missing effective degrees of freedom and introduce physics-informed latent modifications to the generative dynamics of a continuous normalizing flow. We show that these latent corrections recover a substantial fraction of the missing variability without requiring additional training simulations. Using weak-lensing field-level inference as a representative cosmological application, we further demonstrate through posterior analysis that the learned latent directions are actively exploited during inference and behave as effective nuisance parameters that absorb part of the mismatch between the training and target distributions. As a result, the model exhibits improved robustness to out-of-distribution shifts in field-level inference. Our work establishes variance shrinkage as a practical diagnostic for identifying missing degrees of freedom in simulation-trained generative models and provides a framework for recovering them through physics-informed modifications of generative dynamics.

</details>


### [119] [Targeting the future of spectroscopic galaxy surveys -- A cosmological study case with the Wide-field Spectroscopic Telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02828)
*Constantin Payerne,Antoine Rocher,Aurélien Verdier,William d'Assignies,Behnood Bandi,Sofia Contarini,Matteo Ferro,Jean-Paul Kneib,Vincenzo Mainieri,Michele Moresco,Marco Palla,Maxime Rombach,Christophe Yèche,Furkan Akbaba,Hélène Courtois,Darko Donevski,Massimo Guidi,Valentin Ivanov,Konstantinos Migkas,Ivan Minchev,Ignacio del Moral-Castro,Olcay Plevne,Mojtaba Raouf,Laurence Tresse,the WST Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该工作为拟议的12米高复用WST望远镜设计并表征新的目标选择策略，提出MagMax纯星等限制样本（三维色-色星-系分离）和z>2的LBG dropout样本，并用Fisher矩阵评估其对BAO、f、w0wa、f_NL^loc与Σmν的约束力，最终建立把仪器能力与观测时间直接映射到宇宙学约束力的优化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stage-V光谱巡天将观测数亿条星系与类星体光谱，把精密大尺度结构宇宙学拓展到远超Stage-IV的红移和体积；但实现这一潜力需围绕新仪器的深度、复用率和巡天时间重新设计目标选择，而不是简单外推现有方案。

Method: 利用深多波段测光（含CLAUDS与HSC的COSMOS数据），在WST时代可达测光深度下重审SDSS、BOSS/eBOSS、DESI与4MOST-CRS在z<1.6的示踪体选择（亮星系、LRG、ELG、类星体）；引入纯星等限制样本MagMax（三维色-色星-系分离）以及扩展到z>2的LBG dropout样本；用Fisher矩阵预报约束能力。

Result: 给出了WST类巡天对BAO距离参数α_parallel、α_perp、增长率f、演化暗能量w0wa、局域型原初非高斯性f_NL^loc以及中微子质量之和Σmν的约束预报。

Conclusion: 核心成果是一个优化框架，将仪器能力与观测时间直接关联到宇宙学约束力，为未来Stage-V光谱巡天的设计提供科学驱动的模板。

Abstract: The next generation of Stage-V spectroscopic surveys are designed to measure hundreds of millions of galaxy and quasar spectra, extending precision large-scale-structure cosmology to redshifts and volumes well beyond the reach of current Stage-IV facilities. Realising this potential requires revisiting the target selection strategies and designing them explicitly around the depth, multiplexing, and survey time of the new instruments, rather than a direct extrapolation of existing recipes. This work develops and characterises new strategies for the Wide-field Spectroscopic Telescope (WST), a proposed 12-metre, highly multiplexed facility expected to operate in the 2040s. Using deep multi-band photometry, including COSMOS-field data from CLAUDS and HSC, we revisit low-redshift $z<1.6$ tracer selection of SDSS, BOSS/eBOSS, DESI and 4MOST-CRS -- Bright Galaxies, Luminous Red Galaxies, Emission Line Galaxies and quasars -- to photometric depth achievable in the WST era, and introduce a purely magnitude-limited sample (MagMax) exploiting three-dimensional colour-colour star-galaxy separation, and a set of Lyman-Break Galaxy dropout samples extending target selection to $z>2$. Using Fisher-matrix forecasts, we showcase the constraining power of a WST-like cosmological survey on BAO distance parameters $α_\parallel$ and $α_\perp$, the growth rate $f$, the evolving dark energy equation $w_0w_a$, the local-type primordial non-Gaussianity $f_{\rm NL}^{\rm loc}$, and the sum of the neutrino mass $Σm_ν$. The main outcome of this work is an optimisation framework that directly links instrumental capabilities and observing time to cosmological constraining power, providing a science-driven template applicable to the design of future generations of Stage-V spectroscopic surveys.

</details>


### [120] [DBI Softens Stiff-Phase Gravitational-Wave Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03429)
*Samuel Sánchez López,Eemeli Tomberg,Juan C. Bueno Sánchez*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 研究DBI非正则动能项对原初引力波今日密度谱的影响：DBI结构使动能主导阶段的状态方程参数低于正则kination的w=1，从而软化蓝倾并延缓辐射主导，两效应叠加可产生原本不可观测的GW信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则kination（w=1）预测的原初GW谱具有较强蓝倾，其信号往往超出未来实验的探测范围，需考察非正则动能项能否改变这一预言并带来可观测性。

Method: 采用Dirac–Born–Infeld作用量配合runaway势描述标量场动力学：场先驱动暴胀，随后进入动能主导阶段；数值求解背景演化与扰动，从暴胀一直积分到辐射主导时期。

Result: DBI结构降低动能主导阶段的w（相对w=1）从而软化GW谱蓝倾，同时使场能量密度红移变慢、延长stiff阶段；两效应相互作用可给出潜在可观测的GW信号。数值结果显示存在与CMB及BBN约束相容的可行参数空间。

Conclusion: 非正则（DBI）动能项是调节原初GW谱幅度与倾斜的有效机制，可在满足CMB与BBN限制的前提下增强GW信号的实验可探测性。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of non-canonical kinetic terms on the present-day density spectrum of primordial gravitational waves (GWs), by considering the dynamics of a scalar field governed by the Dirac--Born--Infeld (DBI) action and a runaway potential. The field, responsible for a period of inflation, subsequently enters a kinetically dominated regime, where the DBI structure reduces the equation-of-state parameter relative to the canonical kination result $w=1$, thereby softening the blue tilt of the GW spectrum. Simultaneously, the energy density of the field redshifts at a slower rate, delaying radiation domination and prolonging the stiff phase. The interplay between these two effects can lead to a potentially observable GW signal that would otherwise remain beyond the reach of future experiments in the canonical case. We test our predictions by numerically solving the background and perturbations, from inflation until radiation domination, and find viable parameter space compatible with cosmic microwave background and Big Bang Nucleosynthesis constraints.

</details>


### [121] [Multiprobe strategies for Kinematic Lensing with the Roman Space Telescope High Latitude Wide Area Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03497)
*Yu-Hsiu Huang,Jiachuan Xu,Tim Eifler,Elisabeth Krause,Spencer Everett,Fabian Hervas-Peters,Pranjal R. S.,Eric Huff,Yan Xiang Lai*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文基于Roman高纬广域巡天(HLWAS)最新设计的模拟数据，量化运动学引力透镜(KL)及其多探针分析在暗能量约束上的科学回报，KL单独与联合分析分别达到FoM=22.54与34.58。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统弱引力透镜(WL)受形状噪声主导，统计精度受限；HLWAS中重叠的光度与光谱数据覆盖2300平方度，使利用星系运动学抑制形状噪声的运动学引力透镜(KL)成为可能，需评估其宇宙学回报并确定观测策略。

Method: 构建多组模拟分析：利用Deep Tier与早期Medium Tier场数据评估早期KL前景；比较不同旋转角(roll-angle)方案对KL性能的影响；将KL与传统WL进行基准对比；提出联合建模KL、星系成团性与星系-星系透镜的多探针分析；系统误差考虑光度不确定度、剪切标定偏差、星系偏置与重子反馈。

Result: KL单独分析获得暗能量品质因数FoM=22.54，较对应WL分析提升逾两倍；KL多探针方法在采用保守尺度切割条件下将全巡天FoM提升至34.58；现有4次通过、旋转角约0°、10°、170°、180°的HLWAS旋转方案对KL接近最优。

Conclusion: KL可显著超越传统WL的暗能量约束能力，KL多探针联合分析进一步提升科学回报；HLWAS现行旋转角策略对KL表现良好，为Roman早期数据与全巡天宇宙学分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: In its latest design, the High Latitude Wide Area Survey (HLWAS) Medium Tier comprises overlapping photometric and spectroscopic data across 2,300 square degrees. This dataset enables Kinematic Lensing (KL), a Weak Lensing (WL) technique that exploits galaxy kinematics to control for shape noise, the dominant statistical limitation of traditional WL. We present several simulated analyses quantifying the expected cosmological science return of a Roman KL analysis with the latest design of the HLWAS: We explore KL prospects with early Roman data from the Deep Tier fields and from the early Medium Tier fields; we demonstrate the impact of different roll-angle choices on the KL performance; we benchmark KL against traditional WL; we introduce a KL-based multiprobe analysis that jointly models KL, galaxy clustering, and galaxy-galaxy lensing. In these analyses, we consider systematics, including photometric uncertainties, shear calibration bias, galaxy bias, and baryonic feedback. Our results show that KL alone achieves a dark energy figure of merit (FoM) of 22.54, which is more than a factor of two improvement over a corresponding WL analysis. The KL multiprobe approach improves the full-survey FoM to 34.58, even assuming conservative scale cuts in the clustering and galaxy-galaxy lensing. For the HLWAS roll-angle strategy, we find that the current plan of 4 passes with rotations of approximately 0, 10, 170, 180 degrees are near optimal for KL.

</details>


### [122] [Baryonic feedback and intrinsic alignments in profile: a frequentist view of DES Y3 cosmic shear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03537)
*Anoma Ganguly,Elisabeth Krause,Tanvi Karwal,Vivian Miranda*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文用profile likelihood与贝叶斯后验对比分析DES Y3宇宙剪切，揭示$S_8$约束受重子反馈先验体积效应影响，并给出CMB与剪切比数据的缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前宇宙剪切巡天主要约束$S_8$与$\Omega_\mathrm{m}$，其余模型参数受先验主导，导致边缘后验易受先验体积效应影响，尤其通过$S_8$、重子反馈与内禀排列(IA)参数间的简并。作者希望将数据驱动的约束与先验体积效应分离。

Method: 在DES Y3+KiDS-1000 Hybrid模型下，对DES Y3宇宙剪切比较profile likelihood与贝叶斯后验；分别考察仅剪切、加入外部CMB信息、以及加入DES Y3 shear ratios三种情形，并对比TATT与NLA两种IA模型。

Result: 仅用剪切时，profile全局最佳拟合与边缘后验均值在$S_8$上一致，但profile显示反馈强度与$S_8$强简并，$S_8$的$1\sigma$上限由假定的反馈范围决定。加入非$\Omega_\mathrm{m}$、$S_8$的CMB信息可打破简并，抑制先验体积效应并使profile与后验一致；此时小尺度剪切数据偏好与流体模拟及观测气体分数相符的中等重子反馈强度。TATT中额外未约束参数使约束变弱，$S_8$ profile相对NLA发生偏移与展宽；加入shear ratios可缓解简并，使profile与边缘后验高度一致。

Conclusion: 宇宙剪切单独约束$S_8$存在显著先验体积效应，profile likelihood是诊断此类效应的有效工具；外部CMB信息与DES Y3 shear ratios可显著缓解简并，恢复数据驱动的一致约束。

Abstract: Current cosmic shear surveys primarily constrain $S_8$ and $Ω_\mathrm{m}$, while most other model parameters remain dominated by priors. Consequently, marginal posteriors are vulnerable to prior-volume effects, particularly through degeneracies among $S_8$, baryonic feedback, and intrinsic alignment (IA) model parameters. To isolate data-driven constraints from prior volume effects, we compare profile likelihoods with Bayesian posteriors for DES Y3 cosmic shear under the DES Y3+KiDS-1000 Hybrid model. For cosmic shear alone, the profile global best fit and the marginal posterior mean agree in $S_8$, but the profile likelihood reveals a strong degeneracy between feedback strength and $S_8$, leaving the upper $1σ$ limit on $S_8$ determined by the assumed feedback range. External CMB information on parameters other than $Ω_\mathrm{m}$ and $S_8$ breaks this degeneracy, suppressing prior-volume effects and reconciling the profiles with the posteriors. With CMB data included, small-scale cosmic shear data prefers moderate baryonic feedback strengths consistent with hydrodynamical simulations calibrated to observed gas fractions. Furthermore, additional unconstrained parameters in the tidal alignment and tidal torquing (TATT) model result in weaker constraints, shifting and broadening the $S_8$ profile relative to the nonlinear linear alignment (NLA) model. Adding DES Y3 shear ratios mitigates this degeneracy, bringing profile likelihood and marginal posteriors into close agreement.

</details>


### [123] [Toward the one- and two-loop EFTofLSS for Simons Observatory CMB lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03556)
*Evan Saraivanov,Henrique Rubira,Vivian Miranda*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 对Simons Observatory的温度、极化与CMB透镜联合似然做Fisher预报，用一阶与二阶EFTofLSS建模非线性成团，并以主成分导出的单一参数刻画重子反馈，评估其对宇宙学参数偏置与精度的改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMB透镜观测已对非线性结构形成与重子反馈敏感，传统的暗物质-only模型在非线性尺度上会产生参数偏置，需要一个能系统吸收重子效应的理论框架。

Method: 利用无噪声数据向量、设定不同重子反馈情景（以T_AGN为表征），采用一阶与二阶EFTofLSS反项，并对反项做PCA得到一个与AGN有效加热温度T_AGN高度对齐的额外参数，在固定尺度截断下做Fisher矩阵预报。

Result: 仅增加一个参数，一阶与二阶EFTofLSS即可在各类反馈情景下均给出良好拟合；两者都显著降低宇宙学参数偏置，且固定尺度截断时边缘化精度损失很小。一阶EFTofLSS的无偏尺度范围与暗物质-only相当，二阶可延伸至更小尺度并更稳健地兼容不同反馈情景。

Conclusion: 在各自仍保持无偏的最小尺度上评估时，二阶EFTofLSS给出的约束强于暗物质-only模型，说明EFTofLSS是CMB透镜分析中处理重子反馈的可行且高效方案。

Abstract: Cosmic microwave background (CMB) lensing observations are becoming sensitive to non-linear structure formation and baryonic feedback scenarios. The effective field theory of large-scale structure (EFTofLSS) provides a systematic description of matter clustering in the mildly non-linear regime, accounting for baryonic effects through counterterms and offering a consistent framework for cross-correlations with other observables. We perform a Fisher forecast for the joint Simons Observatory temperature, polarization, and CMB lensing likelihood using noiseless data vectors with different baryonic feedback scenarios, modelling non-linear structure formation using the one- and two-loop EFTofLSS. We find that, with a single additional parameter, obtained through a principal-component analysis of the EFTofLSS counterterms, both the one- and two-loop EFTofLSS provide a good fit across the baryonic feedback scenarios considered. This parameter is closely aligned with the effective AGN heating temperature $T_{\rm AGN}$. Both loop orders reduce cosmological parameter biases, with only a small loss in marginalized precision at fixed scale cuts. We find that the one-loop EFTofLSS yields unbiased estimates over a range of scales similar to the dark-matter-only model, while the two-loop EFTofLSS extends this range to smaller scales and accommodates different feedback scenarios more robustly. When each model is evaluated at the smallest scales for which its cosmological parameter estimates remain unbiased, the two-loop EFTofLSS yields stronger constraints than the dark-matter-only model.

</details>


### [124] [Detection of the Galaxy--Galaxy--Temperature Skewed Bispectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03580)
*I. J. Keja,D. Karagiannis,W. R. Coulton,P. D. Meerburg*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 首次测量了星系–星系–温度偏斜双谱（通过unWISE星系密度平方与Planck四个频段交叉相关），信号四频段均为高显著性，频率依赖反映tSZ热气体与CIB尘埃贡献；在固定星系占据下，晕模型拟合显示偏斜双谱所需的积分压力与峰值恒星形成效率低于星系–温度互谱，提示模型缺失质量依赖的气体压力剖面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两点统计（星系–温度互谱）仅利用二点信息，难以同时约束气体压力、CIB发射与星系占据等晕模型描述；需要引入对质量更大、星系对更多的晕赋予更高权重的统计量，以在相近计算成本下提供互补检验。

Method: 将滤波后的unWISE星系过密度的平方与Planck 100/143/217/353 GHz频段图交叉相关，测量星系–星系–温度偏斜双谱；借助多频段频率依赖分离tSZ与CIB贡献，并用固定星系占据的晕模型拟合，与星系–温度互谱结果及Planck成分分离的tSZ、CIB图作对比。

Result: 偏斜双谱在四个频段均被高显著性探测；频率依赖显示tSZ与CIB共同贡献。固定星系占据时，晕模型拟合给出的偏斜双谱积分压力与峰值恒星形成效率低于星系–温度互谱；与成分分离图对比表明该失配并非仅源于频率图拟合。

Conclusion: 该失配说明需采用更灵活的气体压力、CIB发射与星系占据描述，可能指向模型未充分刻画的气体压力剖面的质量依赖；偏斜双谱可作为超越两点统计、计算成本相当的互补检验手段。

Abstract: We present the first measurement of the galaxy--galaxy--temperature skewed bispectrum, obtained by cross-correlating the squared, filtered unWISE galaxy overdensity with \textit{Planck} maps at 100, 143, 217, and 353~GHz. The signal is detected with high significance in all four channels. Its frequency dependence reveals contributions from hot gas through the thermal Sunyaev--Zel'dovich (tSZ) effect and from dust through the cosmic infrared background (CIB). With the galaxy occupation fixed, our halo-model fits favor higher integrated pressure and peak star-formation efficiency for the galaxy--temperature cross-spectra than for the skewed bispectrum. Comparisons with component-separated tSZ and CIB maps from the same \textit{Planck} observations indicate that this mismatch is not confined to the frequency-map fits. This difference motivates testing more flexible descriptions of gas pressure, CIB emission, and galaxy occupation. In particular, it could point to a mass dependence of the gas-pressure profile that our model does not fully capture. By giving greater weight to massive halos that host many galaxy pairs, the skewed bispectrum provides a complementary test of these descriptions, using information beyond two-point statistics at a comparable computational cost.

</details>


### [125] [Mercury (Hg): a relativistic $N$-body simulation for Horndeski gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03637)
*Ahmad Nouri-Zonoz,Farbod Hassani,Julian Adamek,Emilio Bellini,Martin Kunz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: Mercury：Horndeski引力α-基表述下的相对论N体宇宙学模拟代码


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性结构形成模拟中，Horndeski引力的通用线性α-基表述（含时变有效普朗克质量M_*^2与α_K、α_B、α_M、α_T）尚未在相对论N体框架中完整实现；此前工作仅覆盖k-essence与KGB等子类模型，缺乏对一般线性Horndeski理论的非线性处理能力。

Method: 在粒子-网格（PM）方案基础上扩展，加入额外标量自由度的动力学及其有效应力-能量张量，以及用α函数与M_*^2表示的修正爱因斯坦方程；使标量场与非线性物质、度规场协同演化，实现非线性结构形成对标量场演化的源项作用及对引力势的反馈。通过与hi_class接口，支持唯象α函数参数化与协变Horndeski理论（演示nKGB、Cubic Galileon、Brans-Dicke）。线性区与hi_class对比验证，并检验k-essence与KGB的非线性极限。

Result: 在线性区域与hi_class结果一致，并正确恢复非线性k-essence与KGB极限。对光速型（luminal）Horndeski模型，发现演化中的有效普朗克质量通过标量各向异性应力产生引力滑移，并改变控制矢量与张量度规扰动的摩擦项；M_*^2的演化还改变有效暗能量密度扰动的归一化与尺度依赖，在其功率谱上留下相应印记。

Conclusion: Mercury为Horndeski引力的非线性相对论模拟提供了中间步骤：在同一相对论框架内可一致地纳入非线性标量自相互作用与屏蔽效应，为后续完整非线性Horndeski模拟奠定基础。

Abstract: We present Mercury, a relativistic $N$-body code for cosmological simulations in the $α$-basis formulation of Horndeski gravity. Mercury extends a particle-mesh scheme by incorporating the dynamics of the additional scalar degree of freedom and the corresponding modified Einstein equations, expressed in terms of the time-dependent functions $α_{\rm K}, α_{\rm B}, α_{\rm M}, α_{\rm T}$ and the effective Planck mass $M_*^2$. This generalises our previous implementation of Horndeski subclass models, namely $k$-essence and kinetic gravity braiding (KGB), to the general linear Horndeski $α$-basis. The code evolves the additional scalar degree of freedom and its effective stress--energy tensor alongside the nonlinear matter and metric fields, so that nonlinear structure formation sources the scalar-field evolution and feeds back on the gravitational potentials. Through its interface with hi_class, Mercury supports both phenomenological $α$-function parametrisations and covariant Horndeski theories; we demonstrate the latter using nKGB, the Cubic Galileon, and Brans--Dicke models. We validate the implementation against hi_class in the linear regime and recover the expected nonlinear $k$-essence and KGB limits. For the luminal Horndeski models considered here, we show how a running effective Planck mass generates gravitational slip through the scalar anisotropic stress and modifies the friction terms governing the vector and tensor metric perturbations. The evolution of $M_*^2$ also modifies the normalisation and scale dependence of the effective dark-energy density contrast, leaving a corresponding imprint on its power spectrum. The present framework provides an intermediate step towards nonlinear Horndeski simulations, in which nonlinear scalar self-interactions and screening effects can be incorporated consistently within the same relativistic framework.

</details>
